## Summary: How AnyType Syncing Works in OpenClaw Workspace

I've conducted a thorough exploration of the codebase. Here's a comprehensive breakdown of the AnyType syncing system:

---

### **1. SYNC SCRIPTS**

#### **Primary Sync Scripts:**

1. **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/code/anytype-local/sync-to-anytype.js`**
   - HTTP API-based sync for pushing markdown files to AnyType
   - Uses Bearer token authentication
   - Creates AnyType objects (pages) from markdown files
   - Environment variables: `ANYTYPE_API_KEY`, `ANYTYPE_SPACE_ID`, `ANYTYPE_API_URL`
   - Default API URL: `http://127.0.0.1:31012`

2. **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/code/anytype-local/daily-note-generator.js`**
   - Generates daily summary reports
   - Reads OpenClaw workspace memory files
   - Collects git activity
   - Creates timestamped markdown notes and syncs to AnyType via HTTP API

3. **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/skills/anytype-sync/scripts/sync-notes.js`**
   - Session sync for backing up OpenClaw conversation history
   - Supports three modes: backup (one-time), continuous (periodic), export (to file)
   - Reads session history from memory files
   - Default interval: 30 minutes (1800000ms)

4. **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/skills/anytype-sync/scripts/anytype-monitor.js`**
   - AnyType → PostgreSQL monitor and sync
   - Watches AnyType space SQLite databases for changes
   - Syncs objects to PostgreSQL for OpenClaw querying
   - Polls at configurable intervals (default: 60 seconds)

#### **Daily Report Generation:**

**`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh`**
- Bash script for VPS health reporting
- Runs daily at 07:00 JST (22:00 UTC)
- Gathers system metrics: uptime, disk, memory, CPU load
- Collects service status (nginx, fail2ban, anytype-sync, openclaw-gateway)
- Reports security status (fail2ban banned IPs)
- Generates markdown report in `/root/anytype-workspace/VPS-DAILY-REPORT-YYYY-MM-DD.md`
- File watcher automatically picks up and syncs to AnyType

---

### **2. ANYTYPE API/gRPC CLIENT CODE**

#### **Two-Layer Architecture:**

**HTTP API Layer (JavaScript):**
- **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/code/anytype-local/sync-to-anytype.js`** - Simple HTTP client
- **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/skills/anytype-sync/scripts/anytype-api.js`** - Full HTTP API wrapper
  - Methods: `createPage()`, `updatePage()`, `getPage()`, `queryPages()`, `deletePage()`, `listSpaces()`, `getSpace()`, `addTags()`, `removeTags()`, `appendContent()`
  - Handles authentication with Bearer tokens
  - Supports timeout and error handling

**gRPC Layer (Go):**
- **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/code/anytype-local/client.go`** - Core gRPC client
  - Connects to `127.0.0.1:31010` (AnyType local server)
  - Reads session token from `~/.anytype/config.json`
  - Implements automatic token refresh on auth failures
  - Methods: `SyncMarkdownWithID()`, `DeleteMarkdown()`, `OpenSpace()`, `HealthCheck()`
  
- **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/code/anytype-local/api.go`** - RPC implementations
  - `openSpaceRPC()` - Opens space for operations
  - `createObject()` - Creates note objects via `ObjectCreate` RPC
  - `deleteObject()` - Deletes via `ObjectListDelete` RPC
  - `UploadImageWithID()` - Wraps images in markdown notes
  - Error handling with detailed gRPC status codes

---

### **3. CONFIGURATION FILES**

#### **Network Configuration:**
- **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/client-config.yml`**
  - Network ID: `REDACTED`
  - Coordinator peer: `REDACTED`
  - Node addresses: `quic://65.108.24.131:33020` and `65.108.24.131:33010`
  - Node types: coordinator, consensus, tree, file

#### **Environment Variables Required:**
```
ANYTYPE_API_KEY        - Bearer token for HTTP API
ANYTYPE_SPACE_ID       - Space ID (bafyrei...)
ANYTYPE_API_URL        - Default: http://127.0.0.1:31012
MONGODB_URL            - Default: mongodb://127.0.0.1:27017
ANYTYPE_ACCOUNT_ID     - Account identifier
DATABASE_URL           - PostgreSQL connection for monitor
```

---

### **4. MONGODB-RELATED CODE**

#### **Direct MongoDB Access (Go CLI Tool):**

**`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/skills/anytype-sync/cmd/anytype-db/main.go`**

Commands:
- `spaces` - List all spaces in coordinator DB
- `space <id>` - Get space info
- `payloads` - Count total payloads in consensus DB
- `payloads-space <id>` - Get payloads for specific space
- `summary <id>` - Get space summary (count, activity, last updated)
- `activity <id>` - Get recent activity from inboxMessages
- `count [space-id]` - Count payloads (with optional space filter)

MongoDB Structure:
- **Database 1: `coordinator`**
  - Collection: `spaces` - Lists available workspaces
  - Collection: `inboxMessages` - Activity log

- **Database 2: `consensus`**
  - Collection: `payload` - Objects in spaces (keyed by spaceID/)

#### **JavaScript Wrapper:**
- **`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/skills/anytype-sync/scripts/anytype-db.js`** - Node.js wrapper around Go binary

---

### **5. DAILY REPORT GENERATION CODE**

#### **Three-Part System:**

**Part 1: Local Sync Service** (`/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/code/anytype-local/`)
- **`main.go`** - File watcher service
  - Watches `/root/anytype-workspace/` for .md, .jpg, .png, .gif, .webp, .pdf files
  - 2-second debounce period
  - Monitors CREATE, WRITE, REMOVE events
  - Syncs markdown files to AnyType via gRPC

- **`objectmap.go`** - Persistent object ID tracking
  - Maps filenames to AnyType object IDs
  - Stores in `~/.anytype-workspace-objectmap.json`
  - Thread-safe with RWMutex
  - Enables proper deletion (tracks which files map to which objects)

**Part 2: Daily Report Generation** (`vps-daily-report.sh`)
- Gathers system metrics (uptime, disk, memory, load)
- Checks service statuses
- Collects fail2ban statistics
- Generates markdown report
- Writes to `/root/anytype-workspace/VPS-DAILY-REPORT-YYYY-MM-DD.md`

**Part 3: Automatic Sync**
- File watcher detects new markdown file
- gRPC client syncs to AnyType automatically
- Object ID stored for future updates/deletes

#### **Session Sync** (`sync-notes.js`)
- Backs up OpenClaw session to AnyType
- Reads MEMORY.md and daily notes
- Creates timestamped pages
- Can run continuously (every 30 minutes) or on-demand

---

### **6. ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW**

**Complete System Flow:**

```
OpenClaw Bot
    ↓
    ├→ Writes to MongoDB (source of truth)
    └→ Writes to /root/anytype-workspace/*.md (exports)
        ↓
        fsnotify Watcher
        ↓
        anytype-workspace-sync (Go service)
        ├→ Tracks object IDs (.anytype-workspace-objectmap.json)
        └→ gRPC Client (authenticated)
            ↓
            anytype serve -q (local gRPC :31010)
            ├→ Session token from ~/.anytype/config.json
            └→ Auto-refresh on token expiry
                ↓
                Self-Hosted AnyType Network
                ├→ Coordinator Node (MongoDB)
                ├→ Consensus Node (MongoDB)
                └→ File Node (Redis/IPFS)
```

**Key Technologies:**
- **File Watching**: fsnotify (Go)
- **gRPC**: Protocol Buffers for RPC, with authentication metadata
- **MongoDB**: coordinator & consensus databases
- **PostgreSQL**: Optional storage for monitored objects
- **Session Token**: JWT from AnyType config, auto-renewed

**Performance:**
- File detection: <1 second
- Debounce: 2 seconds
- gRPC object creation: 100-300ms
- Total sync latency: ~2-5 seconds

---

### **7. KEY FILES LOCATION MAP**

| Component | Path |
|-----------|------|
| gRPC Sync Service (Go) | `/code/anytype-local/{main.go, client.go, api.go, objectmap.go}` |
| HTTP API Sync | `/code/anytype-local/sync-to-anytype.js` |
| Daily Report | `/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh` |
| MongoDB CLI Tool | `/skills/anytype-sync/cmd/anytype-db/main.go` |
| Session Sync | `/skills/anytype-sync/scripts/sync-notes.js` |
| Workspace Monitor | `/skills/anytype-sync/scripts/anytype-monitor.js` |
| HTTP API Client | `/skills/anytype-sync/scripts/anytype-api.js` |
| Helper Library | `/code/anytype-helper.js` |
| Configuration | `/client-config.yml` |
| Documentation | `/code/anytype-workspace-sync/{ARCHITECTURE.md, SYSTEM_FLOW.md, ...}` |

This is a sophisticated, multi-layered integration system that enables real-time synchronization between OpenClaw, local files, MongoDB, and self-hosted AnyType workspaces with automatic authentication renewal and persistent object tracking.

     1→package main

     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"

    11→	"github.com/fsnotify/fsnotify"

    14→var (
    15→	workspaceDir = os.ExpandEnv("$HOME/anytype-workspace")

    17→	grpcAddr     = "127.0.0.1:31010" // AnyType gRPC port from anytype serve

    22→	debounceTime   = 2 * time.Second
    23→	objectMap      *ObjectMap

    25→
    26→// FileChange represents a markdown file change
    27→type FileChange struct {
    28→	Path     string
    29→	Filename string
    30→	Title    string
    31→	Content  string
    32→}
    33→
    34→// ParseMarkdown extracts title and content from markdown file
    35→func ParseMarkdown(filepath string) (*FileChange, error) {
    36→	content, err := os.ReadFile(filepath)
    37→	if err != nil {
    38→		return nil, err
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	filename := strings.TrimSuffix(filepath[strings.LastIndex(filepath, "/")+1:], ".md")
    42→
    43→	// Extract first heading as title
    44→	title := filename
    45→	lines := strings.Split(string(content), "\n")
    46→	for _, line := range lines {
    47→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "# ") {
    48→			title = strings.TrimPrefix(line, "# ")
    49→			break
    50→		}
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	return &FileChange{
    54→		Path:     filepath,
    55→		Filename: filename,
    56→		Title:    title,
    57→		Content:  string(content),
    58→	}, nil
    59→}
    60→
    61→// SyncFile processes a markdown file for sync
    62→func SyncFile(ctx context.Context, client *AnyTypeClient, filepath string) (string, error) {
    63→	change, err := ParseMarkdown(filepath)
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ✗ Error parsing %s: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filepath, err)
    66→		return "", err
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	fmt.Printf("[%s] Syncing %s...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), change.Filename)
    70→
    71→	// Sync to AnyType via gRPC (if connected)
    72→	if client == nil {
    73→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ⚠ %s queued (gRPC client not connected)\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), change.Filename)
    74→		return "", fmt.Errorf("client not connected")
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Get object ID from the sync operation
    78→	objectID, err := client.SyncMarkdownWithID(ctx, change, spaceID)
    79→	if err != nil {
    80→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ✗ Sync error for %s: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), change.Filename, err)
    81→		return "", err
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	// Store the object ID mapping
    85→	if objectMap != nil {
    86→		if err := objectMap.Set(change.Filename, objectID); err != nil {
    87→			fmt.Printf("[%s] ⚠ Failed to save object mapping: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), err)
    88→		}
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	fmt.Printf("[%s] ✓ %s synced to AnyType\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), change.Filename)
    92→	return objectID, nil
    93→}
    94→

   137→// DeleteFile processes a markdown file deletion
   138→func DeleteFile(ctx context.Context, client *AnyTypeClient, filepath string) {
   139→	filename := strings.TrimSuffix(filepath[strings.LastIndex(filepath, "/")+1:], ".md")
   140→
   141→	fmt.Printf("[%s] Deleting %s...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filename)
   142→
   143→	// Delete from AnyType via gRPC (if connected)
   144→	if client == nil {
   145→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ⚠ %s delete queued (gRPC client not connected)\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filename)
   146→		return
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Get object ID from mapping
   150→	if objectMap == nil {
   151→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ✗ Object map not initialized\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   152→		return
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	objectID, exists := objectMap.Get(filename)
   156→	if !exists {
   157→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ⚠ No object ID found for %s (may have been deleted already)\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filename)
   158→		return
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	if err := client.DeleteMarkdown(ctx, objectID); err != nil {
   162→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ✗ Delete error for %s: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filename, err)
   163→		return
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Remove from object map
   167→	if err := objectMap.Delete(filename); err != nil {
   168→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ⚠ Failed to update object mapping: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), err)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	fmt.Printf("[%s] ✓ %s deleted from AnyType\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filename)
   172→}
   173→
   174→// WatchDirectory monitors for markdown file changes
   175→func WatchDirectory(ctx context.Context, dir string, client *AnyTypeClient, watcher *fsnotify.Watcher) error {
   176→	// Add root directory
   177→	if err := watcher.Add(dir); err != nil {
   178→		return err
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Add subdirectories
   182→	filepath.Walk(dir, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
   183→		if err != nil {
   184→			return err
   185→		}
   186→		if info.IsDir() && path != dir {
   187→			watcher.Add(path)
   188→		}
   189→		return nil
   190→	})
   191→
   192→	fmt.Printf("[%s] Watching %s for changes...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), dir)
   193→
   194→	for {
   195→		select {
   196→		case event, ok := <-watcher.Events:
   197→			if !ok {
   198→				return nil
   199→			}
   200→
   201→	// Only process .md, image, or PDF files
   202→			isMarkdown := strings.HasSuffix(event.Name, ".md")
   203→			isImage := strings.HasSuffix(event.Name, ".jpg") || 
   204→					strings.HasSuffix(event.Name, ".jpeg") ||
   205→					strings.HasSuffix(event.Name, ".png") || 
   206→					strings.HasSuffix(event.Name, ".gif") ||
   207→					strings.HasSuffix(event.Name, ".webp")
   208→			isPDF := strings.HasSuffix(event.Name, ".pdf")
   209→			
   210→			if !isMarkdown && !isImage && !isPDF {
   211→				continue
   212→			}
   213→
   214→			// Check if event is recent (debounce)
   215→			now := time.Now()
   216→			if lastChange, exists := fileTimestamps[event.Name]; exists {
   217→				if now.Sub(lastChange) < debounceTime {
   218→					continue
   219→				}
   220→			}
   221→			fileTimestamps[event.Name] = now
   222→
   223→// Handle different event types
   224→			if event.Op&fsnotify.Remove == fsnotify.Remove {
   225→				// File was deleted
   226→				filename := event.Name[strings.LastIndex(event.Name, "/")+1:]
   227→				if idx := strings.LastIndex(filename, "."); idx != -1 {
   228→					filename = filename[:idx]
   229→				}
   230→				DeleteFile(ctx, client, event.Name)
   231→			} else {
   232→				// Wait for file to stabilize
   233→				time.Sleep(debounceTime)
   234→
   235→// Check if file still exists (for create/write events)
   236→				if _, err := os.Stat(event.Name); err == nil {
   237→					if isMarkdown {
   238→						SyncFile(ctx, client, event.Name)
   239→					} else if isImage || isPDF {
   240→						SyncImage(ctx, client, event.Name)
   241→					}
   242→				}
   243→			}
   244→
   245→		case err, ok := <-watcher.Errors:
   246→			if !ok {
   247→				return nil
   248→			}
   249→			fmt.Printf("[%s] Watcher error: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), err)
   250→		}
   251→	}
   252→}
   253→
   254→// InitialSync syncs all existing markdown files
   255→func InitialSync(ctx context.Context, dir string, client *AnyTypeClient) error {
   256→	fmt.Printf("[%s] Running initial sync...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   257→
   258→	files, err := os.ReadDir(dir)
   259→	if err != nil {
   260→		return err
   261→	}
   262→
   263→for _, file := range files {
   264→		if file.IsDir() {
   265→			continue
   266→		}
   267→		
   268→		filename := file.Name()
   269→		fullPath := filepath.Join(dir, filename)
   270→		
   271→		if strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".md") {
   272→			SyncFile(ctx, client, fullPath)
   273→		} else if strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".jpg") || strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".jpeg") ||
   274→			strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".png") || strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".gif") ||
   275→			strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".webp") || strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".pdf") {
   276→			SyncImage(ctx, client, fullPath)
   277→		}
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	fmt.Printf("[%s] Initial sync complete\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   281→	return nil
   282→}
   283→
   284→func main() {
   285→	ctx := context.Background()
   286→
   287→	// Initialize object map
   288→	var err error
   289→	objectMap, err = NewObjectMap()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Failed to initialize object map: %v\n", err)
   292→		os.Exit(1)
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	// Check workspace directory exists, create if not
   296→	if _, err := os.Stat(workspaceDir); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   297→		fmt.Printf("[%s] Creating workspace directory: %s\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), workspaceDir)
   298→		if err := os.MkdirAll(workspaceDir, 0755); err != nil {
   299→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Error: failed to create %s: %v\n", workspaceDir, err)
   300→			os.Exit(1)
   301→		}
   302→	}
   303→
   304→	// Validate space ID
   305→	if spaceID == "" {
   306→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Error: ANYTYPE_SPACE_ID environment variable is required\n")
   307→		os.Exit(1)
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	// Create file watcher
   311→	watcher, err := fsnotify.NewWatcher()
   312→	if err != nil {
   313→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Failed to create watcher: %v\n", err)
   314→		os.Exit(1)
   315→	}
   316→	defer watcher.Close()
   317→
   318→	// Connect to AnyType gRPC (optional - continue if connection fails)
   319→	fmt.Printf("[%s] Connecting to AnyType at %s...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), grpcAddr)
   320→	client, err := NewAnyTypeClient(grpcAddr)
   321→	if err != nil {
   322→		fmt.Printf("[%s] WARNING: Failed to connect to AnyType: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), err)
   323→		fmt.Printf("[%s] Watcher will run but sync will be disabled until connection succeeds\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   324→		client = nil
   325→	} else {
   326→		defer client.Close()
   327→		fmt.Printf("[%s] Connected to AnyType\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   328→
   329→		// Health check
   330→		if err := client.HealthCheck(ctx); err != nil {
   331→			fmt.Printf("[%s] WARNING: Health check failed: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), err)
   332→		}
   333→
   334→		// Open the space so we can create objects in it
   335→		fmt.Printf("[%s] Opening space %s...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), spaceID)
   336→		if err := client.OpenSpace(ctx, spaceID); err != nil {
   337→			fmt.Printf("[%s] WARNING: Failed to open space: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), err)
   338→			fmt.Printf("[%s] Will continue but sync may fail\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   339→		}
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Initial sync
   343→	if err := InitialSync(ctx, workspaceDir, client); err != nil {
   344→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Initial sync failed: %v\n", err)
   345→		os.Exit(1)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Start watching
   349→	if err := WatchDirectory(ctx, workspaceDir, client, watcher); err != nil {
   350→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Watch failed: %v\n", err)
   351→		os.Exit(1)
   352→	}
   353→}
   354→
```

     1→package main
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"sync"
     9→)
    10→
    11→var objectMapFile = os.ExpandEnv("$HOME/.anytype-workspace-objectmap.json")
    12→
    13→// ObjectMap tracks the mapping between filenames and AnyType object IDs
    14→type ObjectMap struct {
    15→	mu      sync.RWMutex
    16→	mapping map[string]string // filename -> objectID
    17→}
    18→
    19→// NewObjectMap creates a new object map and loads existing mappings
    20→func NewObjectMap() (*ObjectMap, error) {
    21→	om := &ObjectMap{
    22→		mapping: make(map[string]string),
    23→	}
    24→
    25→	// Try to load existing mappings
    26→	if err := om.load(); err != nil {
    27→		// If file doesn't exist, that's ok
    28→		if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    29→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to load object map: %w", err)
    30→		}
    31→	}
    32→
    33→	return om, nil
    34→}
    35→
    36→// Set stores a filename -> objectID mapping
    37→func (om *ObjectMap) Set(filename, objectID string) error {
    38→	om.mu.Lock()
    39→	defer om.mu.Unlock()
    40→
    41→	om.mapping[filename] = objectID
    42→	return om.save()
    43→}
    44→
    45→// Get retrieves the objectID for a filename
    46→func (om *ObjectMap) Get(filename string) (string, bool) {
    47→	om.mu.RLock()
    48→	defer om.mu.RUnlock()
    49→
    50→	objectID, exists := om.mapping[filename]
    51→	return objectID, exists
    52→}
    53→
    54→// Delete removes a filename mapping
    55→func (om *ObjectMap) Delete(filename string) error {
    56→	om.mu.Lock()
    57→	defer om.mu.Unlock()
    58→
    59→	delete(om.mapping, filename)
    60→	return om.save()
    61→}
    62→
    63→// load reads the object map from disk
    64→func (om *ObjectMap) load() error {
    65→	data, err := os.ReadFile(objectMapFile)
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return err
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	return json.Unmarshal(data, &om.mapping)
    71→}
    72→
    73→// save writes the object map to disk
    74→func (om *ObjectMap) save() error {
    75→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(om.mapping, "", "  ")
    76→	if err != nil {
    77→		return err
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	// Ensure directory exists
    81→	dir := filepath.Dir(objectMapFile)
    82→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0755); err != nil {
    83→		return err
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	return os.WriteFile(objectMapFile, data, 0644)
    87→}
    88→
```

     1→package main
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"os/exec"
     9→	"strings"
    10→	"sync"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"google.golang.org/grpc"
    14→	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials/insecure"
    15→	"google.golang.org/grpc/metadata"
    16→)
    17→
    18→// AnyTypeClient wraps gRPC connection to AnyType
    19→type AnyTypeClient struct {
    20→	conn          *grpc.ClientConn
    21→	addr          string
    22→	sessionToken  string
    23→	refreshMutex  sync.Mutex // Prevent concurrent token refreshes
    24→	lastRefresh   time.Time  // Track when we last refreshed
    25→	anytypeBinary string     // Path to anytype binary
    26→}
    27→
    28→// NewAnyTypeClient creates a new gRPC client for AnyType
    29→func NewAnyTypeClient(addr string) (*AnyTypeClient, error) {
    30→	client := &AnyTypeClient{
    31→		addr:          addr,
    32→		anytypeBinary: "/root/.local/bin/anytype", // Default path
    33→	}
    34→
    35→	// Read session token from config
    36→	token, err := readSessionToken()
    37→	if err != nil {
    38→		fmt.Printf("Warning: failed to read session token: %v\n", err)
    39→	} else {
    40→		client.sessionToken = token
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	// Connect to gRPC server
    44→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
    45→	defer cancel()
    46→
    47→	conn, err := grpc.DialContext(
    48→		ctx,
    49→		addr,
    50→		grpc.WithTransportCredentials(insecure.NewCredentials()),
    51→		grpc.WithBlock(),
    52→	)
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to connect to AnyType gRPC: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	client.conn = conn
    58→	return client, nil
    59→}
    60→
    61→// withAuth adds authentication metadata to context
    62→func (c *AnyTypeClient) withAuth(ctx context.Context) context.Context {
    63→	if c.sessionToken != "" {
    64→		return metadata.AppendToOutgoingContext(ctx, "token", c.sessionToken)
    65→	}
    66→	return ctx
    67→}
    68→
    69→// readSessionToken reads the session token from AnyType config
    70→func readSessionToken() (string, error) {
    71→	configPath := os.Getenv("HOME") + "/.anytype/config.json"
    72→
    73→	data, err := os.ReadFile(configPath)
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read config: %w", err)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	var config struct {
    79→		SessionToken string `json:"sessionToken"`
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
    83→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse config: %w", err)
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	return config.SessionToken, nil
    87→}
    88→
    89→// isAuthError checks if an error is an authentication error
    90→func isAuthError(err error) bool {
    91→	if err == nil {
    92→		return false
    93→	}
    94→	errMsg := strings.ToLower(err.Error())
    95→	return strings.Contains(errMsg, "not authenticated") ||
    96→		strings.Contains(errMsg, "authentication failed") ||
    97→		strings.Contains(errMsg, "invalid token") ||
    98→		strings.Contains(errMsg, "session") ||
    99→		strings.Contains(errMsg, "signature is invalid")
   100→}
   101→
   102→// refreshToken attempts to refresh the session token by restarting the anytype server
   103→func (c *AnyTypeClient) refreshToken(ctx context.Context) error {
   104→	c.refreshMutex.Lock()
   105→	defer c.refreshMutex.Unlock()
   106→
   107→	// Check if we recently refreshed (within last 30 seconds)
   108→	// This prevents rapid refresh loops
   109→	if time.Since(c.lastRefresh) < 30*time.Second {
   110→		fmt.Printf("[%s] Token refresh attempted too soon, skipping\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("token refresh rate limit")
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	fmt.Printf("[%s] 🔄 Attempting to refresh session token...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   115→
   116→	// Step 1: Send SIGTERM to existing anytype serve process (graceful shutdown)
   117→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Stopping anytype server...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   118→	killCmd := exec.Command("pkill", "-TERM", "-f", "anytype serve")
   119→	if err := killCmd.Run(); err != nil {
   120→		fmt.Printf("[%s]   ⚠ pkill returned: %v (may be normal)\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), err)
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	// Step 2: Wait for process to fully stop
   124→	time.Sleep(3 * time.Second)
   125→
   126→	// Step 3: Start anytype server in background
   127→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Starting anytype server...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   128→	startCmd := exec.Command("nohup", c.anytypeBinary, "serve", "-q")
   129→
   130→	// Redirect output to log file
   131→	logFile, err := os.OpenFile("/tmp/anytype-serve.log", os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY|os.O_APPEND, 0644)
   132→	if err != nil {
   133→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open log file: %w", err)
   134→	}
   135→	defer logFile.Close()
   136→
   137→	startCmd.Stdout = logFile
   138→	startCmd.Stderr = logFile
   139→
   140→	if err := startCmd.Start(); err != nil {
   141→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to start anytype server: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	// Step 4: Wait for server to initialize and generate new token
   145→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Waiting for server to initialize...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   146→	time.Sleep(10 * time.Second)
   147→
   148→	// Step 5: Verify server is up
   149→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Verifying server is running...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   150→
   151→	// Step 5: Reload session token from config
   152→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Reading new session token...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   153→	newToken, err := readSessionToken()
   154→	if err != nil {
   155→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read new token: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Step 6: Update client's session token
   159→	c.sessionToken = newToken
   160→	c.lastRefresh = time.Now()
   161→
   162→	// Step 7: Reconnect gRPC — the old connection died when anytype restarted
   163→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Reconnecting gRPC to %s...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), c.addr)
   164→	if c.conn != nil {
   165→		c.conn.Close()
   166→	}
   167→	connCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   168→	defer cancel()
   169→	newConn, err := grpc.DialContext(
   170→		connCtx,
   171→		c.addr,
   172→		grpc.WithTransportCredentials(insecure.NewCredentials()),
   173→		grpc.WithBlock(),
   174→	)
   175→	if err != nil {
   176→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to reconnect gRPC after token refresh: %w", err)
   177→	}
   178→	c.conn = newConn
   179→
   180→	fmt.Printf("[%s] ✓ Session token refreshed and gRPC reconnected\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   181→	return nil
   182→}
   183→
   184→// withRetry wraps an operation with automatic token refresh on auth errors
   185→func (c *AnyTypeClient) withRetry(ctx context.Context, operation func() error) error {
   186→	err := operation()
   187→
   188→	// If no error or not an auth error, return immediately
   189→	if err == nil || !isAuthError(err) {
   190→		return err
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	// Auth error detected - try to refresh token
   194→	fmt.Printf("[%s] ⚠ Authentication error detected: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), err)
   195→
   196→	if refreshErr := c.refreshToken(ctx); refreshErr != nil {
   197→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ✗ Token refresh failed: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), refreshErr)
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("auth error (token refresh failed): %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Retry the operation with new token
   202→	fmt.Printf("[%s] 🔁 Retrying operation with refreshed token...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   203→	err = operation()
   204→
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		return fmt.Errorf("operation failed after token refresh: %w", err)
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	return nil
   210→}
   211→
   212→// SyncMarkdown syncs a markdown file to AnyType
   213→func (c *AnyTypeClient) SyncMarkdown(ctx context.Context, change *FileChange, spaceID string) error {
   214→	if c.conn == nil {
   215→		return fmt.Errorf("gRPC client not connected")
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Syncing %s to space %s\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), change.Filename, spaceID)
   219→
   220→	// Use the API layer to sync markdown to AnyType
   221→	_, err := c.SyncMarkdownToAnyType(ctx, change, spaceID)
   222→	return err
   223→}
   224→
   225→// SyncMarkdownWithID syncs a markdown file to AnyType and returns the object ID
   226→func (c *AnyTypeClient) SyncMarkdownWithID(ctx context.Context, change *FileChange, spaceID string) (string, error) {
   227→	if c.conn == nil {
   228→		return "", fmt.Errorf("gRPC client not connected")
   229→	}
   230→
   231→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Syncing %s to space %s\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), change.Filename, spaceID)
   232→
   233→	var objectID string
   234→	var syncErr error
   235→
   236→	// Wrap the sync operation with automatic retry on auth errors
   237→	err := c.withRetry(ctx, func() error {
   238→		objectID, syncErr = c.SyncMarkdownToAnyType(ctx, change, spaceID)
   239→		return syncErr
   240→	})
   241→
   242→	if err != nil {
   243→		return "", err
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	return objectID, nil
   247→}
   248→
   249→// DeleteMarkdown deletes a markdown file from AnyType
   250→func (c *AnyTypeClient) DeleteMarkdown(ctx context.Context, objectID string) error {
   251→	if c.conn == nil {
   252→		return fmt.Errorf("gRPC client not connected")
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Deleting object from AnyType\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   256→
   257→	// Wrap the delete operation with automatic retry on auth errors
   258→	return c.withRetry(ctx, func() error {
   259→		return c.deleteObject(ctx, objectID)
   260→	})
   261→}
   262→
   263→// Close closes the gRPC connection
   264→func (c *AnyTypeClient) Close() error {
   265→	if c.conn != nil {
   266→		return c.conn.Close()
   267→	}
   268→	return nil
   269→}
   270→
   271→// OpenSpace opens/joins a space so we can create objects in it
   272→func (c *AnyTypeClient) OpenSpace(ctx context.Context, spaceID string) error {
   273→	if c.conn == nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("not connected")
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Wrap the open space operation with automatic retry on auth errors
   278→	return c.withRetry(ctx, func() error {
   279→		return c.openSpaceRPC(ctx, spaceID)
   280→	})
   281→}
   282→
   283→// HealthCheck verifies connection to AnyType

   285→	if c.conn == nil {

   290→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Health check placeholder\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   291→	return nil
   292→}

     1→# Setup Hybrid Models — VPS (simplemap.safecast.org)
     2→
     3→## Step 1: SSH into VPS
     4→
     5→```bash
     6→ssh root@65.108.24.131
     7→# or
     8→ssh root@simplemap.safecast.org
     9→```
    10→
    11→---
    12→
    13→## Step 2: Check Current Config
    14→
    15→```bash
    16→cat ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json | head -50
    17→```
    18→
    19→Note: The VPS gateway might have minimal config. That's OK.
    20→
    21→---
    22→
    23→## Step 3: Update OpenClaw Config on VPS
    24→
    25→### Backup current config
    26→```bash
    27→cp ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json.backup
    28→```
    29→
    30→### Edit the config
    31→```bash
    32→nano ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json
    33→```
    34→
    35→Or use your preferred editor. Add/update these sections:
    36→
    37→```json5
    38→{
    39→  "meta": {
    40→    "lastTouchedVersion": "2026.2.26"
    41→  },
    42→  
    43→  "models": {
    44→    "providers": {
    45→      "mistral": {
    46→        "apiKey=[REDACTED]",
    47→        "baseUrl": "https://api.mistral.ai/v1"
    48→      },
    49→      "anthropic": {
    50→        "apiKey=[REDACTED]"
    51→      }
    52→    }
    53→  },
    54→  
    55→  "agents": {
    56→    "defaults": {
    57→      "model": {
    58→        "primary": "mistral/mistral-large",
    59→        "fallbacks": ["anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5-20251001"]
    60→      },
    61→      "workspace": "/root/.openclaw/workspace"
    62→    },
    63→    "mcp": {
    64→      "model": {
    65→        "primary": "anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5-20251001"
    66→      },
    67→      "workspace": "/root/.openclaw/workspace"
    68→    }
    69→  },
    70→  
    71→  "gateway": {
    72→    "port": 18789,
    73→    "mode": "local",
    74→    "bind": "loopback",
    75→    "auth": {
    76→      "mode": "token",
    77→      "token": "your-token-here"
    78→    }
    79→  }
    80→}
    81→```
    82→
    83→### Save and verify
    84→```bash
    85→# Check syntax
    86→cat ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json | jq . > /dev/null && echo "✅ Valid JSON"
    87→```
    88→
    89→---
    90→
    91→## Step 4: Set Environment Variables on VPS
    92→
    93→```bash
    94→# Set Mistral key
    95→export MISTRAL_API_KEY=[REDACTED]"
    96→
    97→# Set Claude key (should already be set)
    98→export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=[REDACTED]"
    99→
   100→# Make permanent
   101→echo 'export MISTRAL_API_KEY=[REDACTED]"' >> /root/.bashrc
   102→echo 'export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=[REDACTED]"' >> /root/.bashrc
   103→source /root/.bashrc
   104→```
   105→
   106→---
   107→
   108→## Step 5: Restart OpenClaw Gateway on VPS
   109→
   110→```bash
   111→systemctl restart openclaw-gateway.service
   112→
   113→# Wait for it to start
   114→sleep 5
   115→
   116→# Check status
   117→systemctl status openclaw-gateway.service --no-pager
   118→```
   119→
   120→You should see:
   121→```
   122→Active: active (running)
   123→```
   124→
   125→---
   126→
   127→## Step 6: Verify Models on VPS
   128→
   129→```bash
   130→# Check if accessible via SSH tunnel
   131→# From your local machine:
   132→ssh -L 18790:localhost:18789 root@65.108.24.131 -N &
   133→sleep 2
   134→
   135→# Test local tunnel
   136→curl http://localhost:18790/api/models 2>/dev/null | jq .
   137→
   138→# Should show both mistral and anthropic models
   139→```
   140→
   141→Or directly on VPS:
   142→```bash
   143→openclaw models list
   144→```
   145→
   146→---
   147→
   148→## Step 7: Test Models on VPS
   149→
   150→### Via SSH tunnel (from local machine)
   151→```bash
   152→# Keep SSH tunnel open in one terminal
   153→ssh -L 18790:localhost:18789 root@65.108.24.131 -N
   154→
   155→# In another terminal:
   156→export OPENCLAW_GATEWAY=http://localhost:18790
   157→openclaw chat "test routine task"
   158→openclaw --agent mcp "test MCP task"
   159→```
   160→
   161→### Directly on VPS
   162→```bash
   163→# SSH into VPS
   164→ssh root@65.108.24.131
   165→
   166→# Test models
   167→openclaw chat --model mistral/mistral-large "test"
   168→openclaw chat --model anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5-20251001 "test"
   169→```
   170→
   171→---
   172→
   173→## Step 8: Test with AnyType Sync Service
   174→
   175→The AnyType sync service should still work:
   176→
   177→```bash
   178→# Check if service is running
   179→systemctl status anytype-workspace-sync.service --no-pager
   180→
   181→# Service should use Claude Haiku (or keep current config)
   182→```
   183→
   184→**Note:** The sync service config is separate. You can update it to use Mistral too if desired.
   185→
   186→---
   187→
   188→## Cost & Usage on VPS
   189→
   190→**VPS traffic pattern:**
   191→- Tablet + local machine → VPS OpenClaw gateway
   192→- Routes to Mistral (cheap) or Haiku (quality)
   193→
   194→**Expected daily cost:**
   195→- 80% routine tasks on Mistral: saves money
   196→- 20% MCP tasks on Haiku: guaranteed to work
   197→- **Total: ~$0.544/day** (vs $3.20/day with 100% Haiku)
   198→
   199→---
   200→
   201→## Monitoring VPS Usage
   202→
   203→```bash
   204→# Check logs
   205→journalctl -u openclaw-gateway.service -f | grep -E "model|mistral|haiku"
   206→
   207→# Check which model is being used
   208→tail -100 /var/log/openclaw.log | grep -i "model\|mistral"
   209→
   210→# Track API calls
   211→# Mistral costs: $0.14/1M input, $0.42/1M output
   212→# Haiku costs: $0.80/1M input, $2.40/1M output
   213→```
   214→
   215→---
   216→
   217→## Troubleshooting on VPS
   218→
   219→### Service won't restart
   220→```bash
   221→# Check for errors
   222→journalctl -u openclaw-gateway.service -n 50 --no-pager
   223→
   224→# Try manual restart
   225→systemctl restart openclaw-gateway.service
   226→sleep 5
   227→systemctl status openclaw-gateway.service
   228→```
   229→
   230→### Models not available
   231→```bash
   232→# Restart and check
   233→systemctl restart openclaw-gateway.service
   234→openclaw models list
   235→
   236→# Check environment variables
   237→env | grep -E "MISTRAL|ANTHROPIC"
   238→```
   239→
   240→### API key errors
   241→```bash
   242→# Verify keys are set
   243→echo $MISTRAL_API_KEY
   244→echo $ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
   245→
   246→# If empty, set them again in /root/.bashrc
   247→nano /root/.bashrc
   248→# Add: export MISTRAL_API_KEY="..."
   249→# Add: export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="..."
   250→
   251→source /root/.bashrc
   252→systemctl restart openclaw-gateway.service
   253→```
   254→
   255→---
   256→
   257→## Verification Checklist
   258→
   259→- [ ] SSH into VPS works
   260→- [ ] Config file updated with both providers
   261→- [ ] Environment variables set
   262→- [ ] OpenClaw gateway restarted
   263→- [ ] Both models show up: `openclaw models list`
   264→- [ ] Test Mistral: `openclaw chat "test"`
   265→- [ ] Test Haiku: `openclaw chat --model anthropic/... "test"`
   266→- [ ] Test MCP agent: `openclaw --agent mcp "test"`
   267→- [ ] AnyType sync service still running
   268→
   269→---
   270→
   271→## Next: Monitor & Optimize
   272→
   273→After setup:
   274→
   275→1. **Use for 1 week** — See actual costs
   276→2. **Check logs** — Confirm model usage patterns
   277→3. **Optimize agent usage** — Make sure MCP tasks use correct agent
   278→4. **Track savings** — Compare vs 100% Haiku
   279→
   280→---
   281→
   282→**Date:** 2026-03-03 13:43 UTC
   283→**Status:** Ready to implement
   284→**Estimated time:** 15 minutes for VPS setup
   285→
```

     1→# Rob's OpenClaw Local Setup — Hybrid Mistral + Haiku
     2→
     3→## Status: ✅ IMPLEMENTED
     4→
     5→**Date:** 2026-03-03
     6→**Machine:** Local (Laptop with RTX 3070)
     7→**Status:** Mistral as primary model
     8→
     9→---
    10→
    11→## Quick Reference
    12→
    13→### Switch Models via Command Line
    14→
    15→**To Mistral (current default):**
    16→```bash
    17→openclaw models set mistral/mistral-large-latest
    18→```
    19→
    20→**To Claude Haiku (when needed):**
    21→```bash
    22→openclaw models set anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5-20251001
    23→```
    24→
    25→**See all available models:**
    26→```bash
    27→openclaw models list --all
    28→```
    29→
    30→**See only configured models:**
    31→```bash
    32→openclaw models list
    33→```
    34→
    35→---
    36→
    37→## Current Setup
    38→
    39→### Models Active
    40→- ✅ **Mistral Large (Latest)** — Primary (cheap, 80-85% quality)
    41→- ✅ **Claude Haiku** — Fallback (quality, native MCP)
    42→
    43→### Cost Configuration
    44→- **Default:** Mistral (~$0.28/1M tokens)
    45→- **Fallback:** Haiku (~$1.60/1M tokens)
    46→- **Expected savings:** ~83% vs 100% Haiku
    47→
    48→---
    49→
    50→## How to Use
    51→
    52→### Routine Tasks (Use Mistral)
    53→```bash
    54→# Default is Mistral
    55→openclaw chat "Write a Python function"
    56→openclaw chat "Explain quantum computing"
    57→```
    58→
    59→### MCP-Heavy Tasks (Switch to Haiku)
    60→```bash
    61→# Switch to Haiku explicitly
    62→openclaw models set anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5-20251001
    63→openclaw chat "Use the safecast MCP to check radiation"
    64→
    65→# Switch back to Mistral after
    66→openclaw models set mistral/mistral-large-latest
    67→```
    68→
    69→### Or Use Agents (Recommended)
    70→```bash
    71→# Default agent uses Mistral
    72→openclaw chat "routine task"
    73→
    74→# MCP agent uses Haiku (if configured)
    75→openclaw --agent mcp "MCP task"
    76→```
    77→
    78→---
    79→
    80→## Configuration Details
    81→
    82→### Current openclaw.json (Local)
    83→
    84→```json5
    85→{
    86→  "models": {
    87→    "default": "mistral/mistral-large-latest",
    88→    "providers": {

    90→        "apiKey=[REDACTED]",

    92→      },

    95→      }
    96→    }

   111→```
   112→
   113→---
   114→
   115→## API Keys Used
   116→

   118→**Anthropic API Key:** `sk-ant-[existing key]`

   125→
   126→---
   127→
   128→## Testing Results
   129→
   130→### Mistral Performance
   131→- ✅ Response time: Fast (~2-3 seconds)
   132→- ✅ Quality: Good for coding & explanations (80-85% of Haiku)
   133→- ✅ Cost: Very low
   134→- ⚠️ MCP support: Via function calling (not native)
   135→
   136→### Haiku Performance
   137→- ✅ Response time: Fast
   138→- ✅ Quality: Excellent (100%)
   139→- ✅ MCP support: Native (works perfectly)
   140→- ❌ Cost: Higher
   141→
   142→---
   143→

   145→
   146→### UI Model Selection (Has Bug)
   147→- ❌ OpenClaw dashboard dropdown under "Model Selection" exists
   148→- ❌ Save button doesn't activate (UI bug)
   149→- ✅ Use command line instead (reliable)

   152→```bash

   155→
   156→# Verify

   158→```
   159→
   160→---
   161→
   162→## Switching Workflow
   163→
   164→**Daily workflow:**
   165→```bash
   166→# 1. Start with Mistral (default)
   167→openclaw chat "routine tasks here"
   168→
   169→# 2. When you need MCP, switch to Haiku

   173→# 3. Switch back to Mistral
   174→openclaw models set mistral/mistral-large-latest
   175→openclaw chat "routine tasks again"
   176→```
   177→
   178→**Or use agents** (less switching):
   179→```bash

   182→
   183→# MCP (mcp agent → Haiku)
   184→openclaw --agent mcp "..."
   185→```
   186→
   187→---
   188→
   189→## Cost Tracking
   190→
   191→### Expected Daily Costs
   192→- Routine work (80% on Mistral): ~$0.224/day
   193→- MCP work (20% on Haiku): ~$0.320/day
   194→- **Total: ~$0.544/day**
   195→
   196→### vs Previous

   198→- **Savings: $2.656/day (83%)**

     1→# Setup Hybrid Models — Local Machine (Your Laptop)
     2→
     3→## Step 1: Get API Keys
     4→

     6→1. Go to https://console.mistral.ai

   104→## Step 3: Set Environment Variables
   105→
   106→```bash

   108→export MISTRAL_API_KEY=[REDACTED]"
   109→
   110→# Claude should already be set, but verify

   112→

   115→echo 'export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=[REDACTED]"' >> ~/.bashrc
   116→source ~/.bashrc

   121→## Step 4: Restart OpenClaw Gateway
   122→
   123→```bash

   125→```
   126→

   130→```
   131→

   133→```

   140→

   143→```

   150→```
   151→
   152→---
   153→
   154→## Step 6: Test Both Models
   155→
   156→### Test Mistral (routine chat)
   157→```bash
   158→openclaw chat "What is 2+2? Answer briefly."
   159→```
   160→
   161→Should respond quickly and cheaply ✅
   162→
   163→### Test Haiku (high-quality response)
   164→```bash
   165→openclaw chat --model anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5-20251001 "Explain quantum computing in 2 sentences"
   166→```
   167→
   168→Should give detailed, high-quality response ✅
   169→
   170→### Test MCP Agent
   171→```bash

   173→```
   174→
   175→Should use Haiku automatically ✅
   176→
   177→---
   178→
   179→## Step 7: Test with MCP Tools
   180→
   181→When you have MCP configured:
   182→

   185→openclaw chat "write a python function"
   186→
   187→# MCP work (uses Haiku - mcp agent)

   189→```
   190→
   191→---
   192→
   193→## Troubleshooting
   194→
   195→### Models not showing up
   196→```bash
   197→# Force reload
   198→openclaw gateway restart
   199→openclaw models list
   200→```
   201→
   202→### API key errors
   203→```bash
   204→# Check environment variables

   206→echo $ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
   207→
   208→# If empty, set them again

   212→
   213→### Model fails to respond
   214→```bash
   215→# Check logs

   217→
   218→# Try other model

     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→
     3→# VPS Daily Health Report - Generates report and syncs to AnyType
     4→# Runs: Daily at 07:00 JST (22:00 UTC)
     5→
     6→REPORT_DIR="/root/anytype-workspace"
     7→TIMESTAMP=$(date +'%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')
     8→DATE=$(date +'%Y-%m-%d')
     9→REPORT_FILE="$REPORT_DIR/VPS-DAILY-REPORT-$DATE.md"
    10→
    11→# Gather system info
    12→UPTIME=$(uptime | sed 's/.*up //' | sed 's/,.*//')
    13→DISK=$(df -h / | tail -1 | awk '{print $3 "/" $2 " (" $5 ")"}')
    14→MEMORY=$(free -h | grep Mem | awk '{print $3 "/" $2}')
    15→LOAD=$(cat /proc/loadavg | awk '{print $1 ", " $2 ", " $3}')
    16→
    17→# Service status
    18→NGINX_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active nginx)
    19→FAIL2BAN_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active fail2ban)
    20→ANYTYPE_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active anytype-workspace-sync)
    21→OPENCLAW_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active openclaw-gateway)
    22→
    23→# fail2ban stats
    24→BANNED_IPS=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null | grep "Currently banned" | awk '{print $NF}' || echo "0")
    25→TOTAL_BANNED=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null | grep "Total banned" | awk '{print $NF}' || echo "0")
    26→
    27→# AnyType workspace status
    28→ANYTYPE_FILES=$(ls -1 /root/anytype-workspace/*.{md,jpg,png,pdf} 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
    29→
    30→# Generate report
    31→cat > "$REPORT_FILE" << EOF
    32→# VPS Daily Health Report — $DATE
    33→
    34→**Generated:** $TIMESTAMP
    35→**Report Time:** 07:00 JST / 22:00 UTC
    36→
    37→---
    38→
    39→## System Status
    40→
    41→### Uptime
    42→\`\`\`
    43→$UPTIME
    44→\`\`\`
    45→
    46→### Resources
    47→- **Disk:** $DISK
    48→- **Memory:** $MEMORY
    49→- **CPU Load:** $LOAD (1m, 5m, 15m)
    50→
    51→---
    52→
    53→## Services
    54→
    55→| Service | Status | Uptime |
    56→|---------|--------|--------|
    57→| nginx | ✅ $NGINX_STATUS | Running |
    58→| fail2ban | ✅ $FAIL2BAN_STATUS | Running |
    59→| anytype-workspace-sync | ✅ $ANYTYPE_STATUS | Running |
    60→| openclaw-gateway | ✅ $OPENCLAW_STATUS | Running |
    61→
    62→---
    63→
    64→## Security
    65→
    66→**fail2ban:**
    67→- Currently banned IPs: $BANNED_IPS
    68→- Total banned (all time): $TOTAL_BANNED
    69→- Status: ✅ Active & protecting
    70→
    71→---
    72→
    73→## Storage
    74→
    75→**AnyType Workspace:**
    76→- Files synced: $ANYTYPE_FILES
    77→- Status: ✅ Healthy
    78→
    79→---
    80→
    81→## Summary
    82→
    83→✅ **All systems operational**
    84→✅ **No critical alerts**
    85→✅ **Security active**
    86→✅ **Storage healthy**
    87→
    88→---
    89→
    90→**Next report:** $(date -d '+1 day' +'%Y-%m-%d 07:00 JST')
    91→EOF
    92→
    93→echo "✓ Report generated: $REPORT_FILE"
    94→
    95→# Sync to AnyType (if it exists, file watcher will pick it up)
    96→# No action needed - file watcher monitors the directory
    97→
    98→exit 0
    99→
```

     1→package main
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"time"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/anyproto/anytype-heart/pb"
     9→	"github.com/anyproto/anytype-heart/pb/service"
    10→	"github.com/gogo/protobuf/types"
    11→	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
    12→	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// openSpaceRPC opens a space via gRPC so we can create objects in it
    16→func (c *AnyTypeClient) openSpaceRPC(ctx context.Context, spaceID string) error {
    17→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Opening workspace/space %s...\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), spaceID)
    18→
    19→	// Create gRPC client stub
    20→	client := service.NewClientCommandsClient(c.conn)
    21→
    22→	// Add authentication to context
    23→	ctx = c.withAuth(ctx)
    24→
    25→	// Create request
    26→	req := &pb.RpcWorkspaceOpenRequest{
    27→		SpaceId: spaceID,
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	// Call WorkspaceOpen RPC
    31→	resp, err := client.WorkspaceOpen(ctx, req)
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		return c.handleGRPCError(err)
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	// Check response error
    37→	if resp.Error != nil && resp.Error.Code != pb.RpcWorkspaceOpenResponseError_NULL {
    38→		return fmt.Errorf("WorkspaceOpen failed: %s (%s)", resp.Error.Description, resp.Error.Code)
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Workspace/space opened successfully\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
    42→	return nil
    43→}
    44→
    45→// SyncMarkdownToAnyType creates or updates a page in AnyType from markdown
    46→func (c *AnyTypeClient) SyncMarkdownToAnyType(ctx context.Context, change *FileChange, spaceID string) (string, error) {
    47→	if c.conn == nil {
    48→		return "", fmt.Errorf("not connected to AnyType")
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Creating/updating '%s' in AnyType\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), change.Title)
    52→
    53→	// Create a new object (page) in the space with title and content
    54→	objectID, err := c.createObject(ctx, change.Title, change.Content, spaceID)
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create object: %w", err)
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Created/updated object '%s' successfully (ID: %s)\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), change.Title, objectID)
    60→	return objectID, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// createObject invokes ObjectCreate RPC to create a new AnyType object
    64→func (c *AnyTypeClient) createObject(ctx context.Context, title string, content string, spaceID string) (string, error) {
    65→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Creating object: title='%s', content_len=%d bytes\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), title, len(content))
    66→
    67→	// Create gRPC client stub
    68→	client := service.NewClientCommandsClient(c.conn)
    69→
    70→	// Add authentication to context
    71→	ctx = c.withAuth(ctx)
    72→
    73→	// Create details struct with title and content
    74→	details := &types.Struct{
    75→		Fields: map[string]*types.Value{
    76→			"name": {
    77→				Kind: &types.Value_StringValue{
    78→					StringValue: title,
    79→				},
    80→			},
    81→			"description": {
    82→				Kind: &types.Value_StringValue{
    83→					StringValue: content,
    84→				},
    85→			},
    86→		},
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	// Create request with space ID and object details
    90→	// Use "ot-note" as the object type (AnyType note type)
    91→	req := &pb.RpcObjectCreateRequest{
    92→		SpaceId:              spaceID,
    93→		Details:              details,
    94→		ObjectTypeUniqueKey:  "ot-note",
    95→		InternalFlags:        nil,
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Call ObjectCreate RPC
    99→	resp, err := client.ObjectCreate(ctx, req)
   100→	if err != nil {
   101→		return "", c.handleGRPCError(err)
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	// Check response error
   105→	if resp.Error != nil && resp.Error.Code != pb.RpcObjectCreateResponseError_NULL {
   106→		return "", fmt.Errorf("ObjectCreate failed: %s (%s)", resp.Error.Description, resp.Error.Code)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	objectID := resp.ObjectId
   110→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Created object ID: %s\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), objectID)
   111→	return objectID, nil
   112→}
   113→
   114→// deleteObject invokes ObjectListDelete RPC to delete an AnyType object
   115→func (c *AnyTypeClient) deleteObject(ctx context.Context, objectID string) error {
   116→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Deleting object ID: %s\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), objectID)
   117→
   118→	// Create gRPC client stub
   119→	client := service.NewClientCommandsClient(c.conn)
   120→
   121→	// Add authentication to context
   122→	ctx = c.withAuth(ctx)
   123→
   124→	// Create request to delete the object
   125→	req := &pb.RpcObjectListDeleteRequest{
   126→		ObjectIds: []string{objectID},
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Call ObjectListDelete RPC
   130→	resp, err := client.ObjectListDelete(ctx, req)
   131→	if err != nil {
   132→		return c.handleGRPCError(err)
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	// Check response error
   136→	if resp.Error != nil && resp.Error.Code != pb.RpcObjectListDeleteResponseError_NULL {
   137→		return fmt.Errorf("ObjectListDelete failed: %s (%s)", resp.Error.Description, resp.Error.Code)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	fmt.Printf("[%s]   → Successfully deleted object\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339))
   141→	return nil
   142→}
   143→
   144→// UploadImageWithID creates an image/file object in AnyType from binary data
   145→// For now, wraps images in markdown notes (simpler approach)
   146→func (c *AnyTypeClient) UploadImageWithID(ctx context.Context, filename string, data []byte, spaceID string) (string, error) {
   147→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Uploading image '%s' (%d bytes)\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filename, len(data))
   148→
   149→	// Create a markdown note documenting the image
   150→	markdown := fmt.Sprintf(`# Image: %s
   151→
   152→**Size:** %d bytes
   153→**Uploaded:** %s
   154→
   155→[Image file - %d bytes]
   156→`, filename, len(data), time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), len(data))
   157→
   158→	// Create as markdown note
   159→	change := &FileChange{
   160→		Path:     fmt.Sprintf("/root/anytype-workspace/%s.md", filename),
   161→		Filename: filename,
   162→		Title:    fmt.Sprintf("Image: %s", filename),
   163→		Content:  markdown,
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	objectID, err := c.SyncMarkdownWithID(ctx, change, spaceID)
   167→	if err != nil {
   168→		fmt.Printf("[%s] ✗ Image sync error for %s: %v\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filename, err)
   169→		return "", err
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	fmt.Printf("[%s] gRPC: Image '%s' uploaded successfully (ID: %s)\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), filename, objectID)
   173→	return objectID, nil
   174→}
   175→
   176→// handleGRPCError provides detailed error messages for gRPC failures
   177→func (c *AnyTypeClient) handleGRPCError(err error) error {
   178→	if err == nil {
   179→		return nil
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	st, ok := status.FromError(err)
   183→	if !ok {
   184→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown gRPC error: %w", err)
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	switch st.Code() {
   188→	case codes.OK:
   189→		return nil
   190→	case codes.Unavailable:
   191→		return fmt.Errorf("AnyType service unavailable - check if AnyType is running")
   192→	case codes.DeadlineExceeded:
   193→		return fmt.Errorf("gRPC call timeout")
   194→	case codes.PermissionDenied:
   195→		return fmt.Errorf("permission denied - check credentials or space access")
   196→	case codes.NotFound:
   197→		return fmt.Errorf("space or object not found")
   198→	case codes.InvalidArgument:
   199→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid request: %s", st.Message())
   200→	case codes.Internal:
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("AnyType internal error: %s", st.Message())
   202→	case codes.Unauthenticated:
   203→		return fmt.Errorf("not authenticated - check network membership")
   204→	default:
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("gRPC error %s: %s", st.Code(), st.Message())
   206→	}
   207→}
   208→

     3→/**
     4→ * AnyType HTTP API Client
     5→ * 
     6→ * Wrapper for AnyType's HTTP API with auth, error handling, and convenience methods
     7→ * for common operations (create page, query, update, etc.)
     8→ * 
     9→ * Usage:
    10→ *   const anytype = require('./anytype-api.js');
    11→ *   const client = anytype.createClient({ apiUrl, apiKey });
    12→ *   await client.createPage({ spaceId, title, content });

    14→
    15→const https = require('https');
    16→const http = require('http');
    17→const { URL } = require('url');
    18→
    19→class AnytypeClient {
    20→  constructor(config = {}) {
    21→    this.apiUrl = config.apiUrl || process.env.ANYTYPE_API_URL || 'http://127.0.0.1:31012';
    22→    this.apiKey = config.apiKey || process.env.ANYTYPE_API_KEY || '';
    23→    this.spaceId = config.spaceId || process.env.ANYTYPE_SPACE_ID || '';
    24→    this.timeout = config.timeout || 30000;
    25→    
    26→    if (!this.apiKey) {
    27→      throw new Error('AnyType API key is required. Set via config.apiKey, ANYTYPE_API_KEY env var, or --apiKey flag');
    28→    }
    29→  }
    30→
    31→  /**
    32→   * Make authenticated HTTP request to AnyType API
    33→   */
    34→  async request(method, path, data = null) {
    35→    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    36→      const url = new URL(path, this.apiUrl);
    37→      const isHttps = url.protocol === 'https:';
    38→      const httpLib = isHttps ? https : http;
    39→
    40→      const headers = {
    41→        'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
    42→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    43→        'User-Agent': 'OpenClaw/1.0'
    44→      };
    45→
    46→      const options = {
    47→        method,
    48→        headers,
    49→        timeout: this.timeout
    50→      };
    51→
    52→      const req = httpLib.request(url, options, (res) => {
    53→        let body = '';
    54→
    55→        res.on('data', (chunk) => {
    56→          body += chunk;
    57→        });
    58→
    59→        res.on('end', () => {
    60→          try {
    61→            // Handle non-JSON responses
    62→            let parsed;
    63→            try {
    64→              parsed = body ? JSON.parse(body) : {};
    65→            } catch (e) {
    66→              parsed = { raw: body };
    67→            }
    68→
    69→            if (res.statusCode >= 400) {
    70→              const error = new Error(
    71→                `AnyType API error (${res.statusCode}): ${parsed.error || parsed.message || body}`
    72→              );
    73→              error.statusCode = res.statusCode;
    74→              error.response = parsed;
    75→              reject(error);
    76→            } else {
    77→              resolve(parsed);
    78→            }
    79→          } catch (e) {
    80→            reject(e);
    81→          }
    82→        });
    83→      });
    84→
    85→      req.on('error', reject);
    86→      req.on('timeout', () => {
    87→        req.abort();
    88→        reject(new Error(`AnyType API request timeout (${this.timeout}ms)`));
    89→      });
    90→
    91→      if (data) {
    92→        req.write(JSON.stringify(data));
    93→      }
    94→
    95→      req.end();
    96→    });
    97→  }

   102→  async createPage({ spaceId = this.spaceId, title, content, type = 'page', tags = [] }) {

   104→    if (!title) throw new Error('title is required');

   129→

   177→  async deletePage({ spaceId = this.spaceId, pageId }) {

   194→  async getSpace(spaceId) {

   216→  async removeTags({ spaceId = this.spaceId, pageId, tags = [] }) {

   230→  async appendContent({ spaceId = this.spaceId, pageId, content }) {

   290→      `);
   291→      process.exit(0);

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. Let me lay out what I've found and propose a solution.

## Root Cause of the Duplicates

The sync bug is in [api.go:46-61](code/anytype-local/api.go#L46-L61) - `SyncMarkdownToAnyType()` **always calls `createObject()`**, never updates an existing one. Even though the `objectMap` tracks filename→objectID mappings, the sync function ignores it. Every time:

1. The service restarts → `InitialSync` re-creates ALL files as new objects
2. A file is modified → a **new** object is created instead of updating the existing one
3. The daily report at 7:01 → creates a new duplicate every time it runs

When OpenClaw tried to fix this by writing directly to MongoDB, it added even more duplicates since AnyType's MongoDB is an internal data store, not meant for direct writes.

## MCP vs Current Sync - Your Instinct is Right

Looking at yesterday's setup in [settings.local.json](/.claude/settings.local.json#L43-L44), you got `@anyproto/anytype-mcp` (the official AnyType MCP server) working via SSH tunnel. This is a much better approach than the custom gRPC sync because:

| | Current gRPC Sync | AnyType MCP |
|---|---|---|
| Maintained by | Us (custom Go code) | Anytype team (official) |
| CRUD | Only creates, never updates | Full create/read/update/delete |
| Duplicates | Creates them constantly | Handles properly |
| Complexity | Go binary + file watcher + objectmap + token refresh | Single `npx` command |
| Daily reports | Broken (causes duplicates) | Would work correctly |

**My recommendation:** Switch the VPS to use AnyType MCP instead of the custom sync. The daily report script would just call the MCP server to create/update the report, and OpenClaw on the VPS would have native AnyType access.

Before I write a plan, two questions:

=== ANYTYPE-CLI SERVICE ===
[Unit]
Description=Anytype CLI headless server
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=root
ExecStartPre=/usr/local/bin/anytype-prestart.sh
ExecStartPre=/bin/sh -c '! ss -tln | grep -q ":31010" || exit 1'
ExecStart=/root/.local/bin/anytype serve -q

# Process management (avoid zombies)
KillMode=mixed
KillSignal=SIGTERM
TimeoutStopSec=60

# Auto-restart only on failure, not if port already in use
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10
StartLimitInterval=600
StartLimitBurst=5

# Resource limits
MemoryMax=500M
CPUQuota=50%

# Security
PrivateTmp=yes
NoNewPrivileges=true

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
=== ANYTYPE SERVICE ===
[Unit]
Description=Anytype Self-Hosted Sync Server
After=network.target mongod.service redis-stack-server.service
Requires=mongod.service redis-stack-server.service

[Service]
Type=simple
User=anytype
Group=anytype
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/any-sync-bundle start-bundle --bundle-config /var/lib/anytype/bundle-config.yml --client-config /var/lib/anytype/data/client-config.yml
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
=== ANYTYPE WORKSPACE SYNC SERVICE ===
[Unit]
Description=AnyType Workspace File Watcher (Go)
After=network.target anytype-cli.service
Wants=anytype-cli.service

[Service]
Type=simple
User=root
WorkingDirectory=/root/anytype-workspace
ExecStart=/root/anytype-workspace-sync-bin
Restart=always
RestartSec=5
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal
SyslogIdentifier=anytype-workspace-sync

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target

=== PRESTART SCRIPT ===
#!/bin/bash
# Pre-start cleanup: Wait for anytype ports to be released

for i in {1..30}; do
    # Check if ports are in use
    if ! netstat -tlnp 2>/dev/null | grep -E "31010|31011"; then
        exit 0
    fi
    echo "Waiting for ports 31010-31011... ($i/30)"
    sleep 1
done

# Force kill if still bound
netstat -tlnp 2>/dev/null | grep -E "31010|31011" && fuser -k 31010/tcp 31011/tcp 2>/dev/null || true
sleep 2
exit 0

Ubuntu 24.04.4 LTS
=== WATCHER SCRIPT ===
#!/bin/bash
WATCH_DIR="/root/anytype-workspace"
LOG_FILE="/var/log/anytype-watcher.log"
SYNC_CLIENT="/root/anytype-sync/sync-client"
export PATH=$PATH:/usr/local/go/bin

log() {
    echo "[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] $1" | tee -a $LOG_FILE
}

log "Starting AnyType workspace watcher for: $WATCH_DIR"

# Only watch for .md files
inotifywait -m -r -e create,moved_to $WATCH_DIR --include '.*\.md$' --format '%w%f %e' |
while read -r file event; do
    log "Detected $event: $file"
    
    # Give the file a moment to finish writing
    sleep 0.5
    
    # Sync to AnyType
    if [ -f "$file" ]; then
        log "Syncing to AnyType: $file"
        $SYNC_CLIENT "$file" >> $LOG_FILE 2>&1
        if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
            log "✅ Successfully synced: $(basename $file)"
        else
            log "❌ Failed to sync: $(basename $file)"
        fi
    fi
done

# Only watch for .md files

while read -r file event; do
    log "Detected $event: $file"

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/.claude/plans/magical-drifting-marshmallow.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Switch VPS AnyType Sync from gRPC to MCP\n\n## Context\n\nThe VPS (simplemap.safecast.org / 65.108.24.131) has a custom Go file-watcher service (`anytype-workspace-sync`) that syncs markdown files to AnyType via gRPC. This service has a critical bug: `SyncMarkdownToAnyType()` in `api.go` **always creates new objects** instead of updating existing ones, causing duplicates on every service restart and file modification.\n\nYesterday, the official AnyType MCP server (`@anyproto/anytype-mcp`) was successfully set up on the laptop via SSH tunnel. The MCP approach is maintained by Anytype, handles CRUD properly, and is much simpler than the custom Go sync.\n\n**Current state on VPS:**\n- `anytype serve -q` running on gRPC port 31010\n- HTTP API port 31012 **NOT listening** (needed for MCP)\n- `anytype-workspace-sync.service` active but failing with auth/space errors\n- `anytype-cli.service` inactive (dead)\n- 43 entries in objectmap, unknown number of duplicates in AnyType\n- No cron job for daily reports (was relying on file watcher)\n\n## Plan\n\n### Step 1: Enable the HTTP API on anytype-cli\n\nModify `/etc/systemd/system/anytype-cli.service` to ensure `anytype serve` also exposes the HTTP API on port 31012 (it's the default `--listen-address` but may need explicit start).\n\nThe current `anytype serve -q` is running (started by the sync service's token refresh), but without the HTTP API. We need to:\n1. Stop the rogue `anytype serve` process\n2. Stop `anytype-workspace-sync.service`\n3. Start `anytype-cli.service` properly (which will start `anytype serve -q` with HTTP API)\n\n### Step 2: Verify HTTP API works\n\n```bash\ncurl -H \"Authorization: Bearer <token>\" http://127.0.0.1:31012/api/v1/spaces\n```\n\nGet the session token from `/root/.anytype/config.json` and the API key.\n\n### Step 3: Clean up duplicates in AnyType\n\nUse the HTTP API to:\n1. Query all objects in the space\n2. Identify duplicates (same title/name)\n3. Keep the most recent version, delete the rest\n4. Write a cleanup script using the HTTP API\n\n### Step 4: Set up AnyType MCP on the VPS\n\nInstall `@anyproto/anytype-mcp` and configure it for OpenClaw on the VPS:\n\n```bash\nnpx -y @anyproto/anytype-mcp\n```\n\nConfigure in OpenClaw's MCP settings (or directly as environment).\n\n### Step 5: Replace daily report mechanism\n\nReplace the file-watcher approach with a cron job that:\n1. Generates the daily report markdown (reuse existing `vps-daily-report.sh`)\n2. Uses the AnyType HTTP API directly to create/update the report\n3. Checks if today's report already exists (by name) and updates instead of creating\n\nCreate a new script: `/root/scripts/daily-report-mcp.sh`\n\n### Step 6: Disable old sync services\n\n```bash\nsystemctl stop anytype-workspace-sync.service\nsystemctl disable anytype-workspace-sync.service\nsystemctl stop anytype-watcher.service\nsystemctl disable anytype-watcher.service\n```\n\n### Step 7: Set up the cron job\n\n```bash\n# Run daily at 22:01 UTC (07:01 JST)\necho \"1 22 * * * /root/scripts/daily-report-mcp.sh\" | crontab -\n```\n\n## Key Files\n\n- VPS services: `/etc/systemd/system/anytype-cli.service`, `anytype-workspace-sync.service`, `anytype-watcher.service`\n- AnyType config: `/root/.anytype/config.json` (session token)\n- Object map: `/root/.anytype-workspace-objectmap.json`\n- Daily report: `/root/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh` (existing, to be adapted)\n- Workspace dir: `/root/anytype-workspace/`\n- OpenClaw config: `/root/.openclaw/openclaw.json`\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `curl http://127.0.0.1:31012/api/v1/spaces` returns space list\n2. Duplicates removed from AnyType (check via API query)\n3. Daily report creates/updates correctly at 07:01 JST\n4. Old sync services disabled and not running\n5. AnyType MCP accessible via OpenClaw on VPS\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Switch VPS AnyType Sync from gRPC to MCP

## Context

The VPS (simplemap.safecast.org / 65.108.24.131) has a custom Go file-watcher service (`anytype-workspace-sync`) that syncs markdown files to AnyType via gRPC. This service has a critical bug: `SyncMarkdownToAnyType()` in `api.go` **always creates new objects** instead of updating existing ones, causing duplicates on every service restart and file modification.

Yesterday, the official AnyType MCP server (`@anyproto/anytype-mcp`) was successfully set up on the laptop via SSH tunnel. The MCP approach is maintained by Anytype, handles CRUD properly, and is much simpler than the custom Go sync.

**Current state on VPS:**
- `anytype serve -q` running on gRPC port 31010
- HTTP API port 31012 **NOT listening** (needed for MCP)
- `anytype-workspace-sync.service` active but failing with auth/space errors
- `anytype-cli.service` inactive (dead)
- 43 entries in objectmap, unknown number of duplicates in AnyType
- No cron job for daily reports (was relying on file watcher)

## Plan

### Step 1: Enable the HTTP API on anytype-cli

Modify `/etc/systemd/system/anytype-cli.service` to ensure `anytype serve` also exposes the HTTP API on port 31012 (it's the default `--listen-address` but may need explicit start).

The current `anytype serve -q` is running (started by the sync service's token refresh), but without the HTTP API. We need to:
1. Stop the rogue `anytype serve` process
2. Stop `anytype-workspace-sync.service`
3. Start `anytype-cli.service` properly (which will start `anytype serve -q` with HTTP API)

### Step 2: Verify HTTP API works

```bash
curl -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" http://127.0.0.1:31012/api/v1/spaces
```

Get the session token from `/root/.anytype/config.json` and the API key.

### Step 3: Clean up duplicates in AnyType

Use the HTTP API to:
1. Query all objects in the space
2. Identify duplicates (same title/name)
3. Keep the most recent version, delete the rest
4. Write a cleanup script using the HTTP API

### Step 4: Set up AnyType MCP on the VPS

Install `@anyproto/anytype-mcp` and configure it for OpenClaw on the VPS:

```bash
npx -y @anyproto/anytype-mcp
```

Configure in OpenClaw's MCP settings (or directly as environment).

### Step 5: Replace daily report mechanism

Replace the file-watcher approach with a cron job that:
1. Generates the daily report markdown (reuse existing `vps-daily-report.sh`)
2. Uses the AnyType HTTP API directly to create/update the report
3. Checks if today's report already exists (by name) and updates instead of creating

Create a new script: `/root/scripts/daily-report-mcp.sh`

### Step 6: Disable old sync services

```bash
systemctl stop anytype-workspace-sync.service
systemctl disable anytype-workspace-sync.service
systemctl stop anytype-watcher.service
systemctl disable anytype-watcher.service
```

### Step 7: Set up the cron job

```bash
# Run daily at 22:01 UTC (07:01 JST)
echo "1 22 * * * /root/scripts/daily-report-mcp.sh" | crontab -
```

## Key Files

- VPS services: `/etc/systemd/system/anytype-cli.service`, `anytype-workspace-sync.service`, `anytype-watcher.service`
- AnyType config: `/root/.anytype/config.json` (session token)
- Object map: `/root/.anytype-workspace-objectmap.json`
- Daily report: `/root/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh` (existing, to be adapted)
- Workspace dir: `/root/anytype-workspace/`
- OpenClaw config: `/root/.openclaw/openclaw.json`

## Verification

1. `curl http://127.0.0.1:31012/api/v1/spaces` returns space list
2. Duplicates removed from AnyType (check via API query)
3. Daily report creates/updates correctly at 07:01 JST
4. Old sync services disabled and not running
5. AnyType MCP accessible via OpenClaw on VPS

There are 78 Radnote sensors (Notehub-connected devices) deployed across Ukraine. These are compact IoT radiation monitors that automatically transmit measurements via cellular or satellite networks. Many of these devices reported data as recently as March 2026, indicating that a large portion of the network remains operational despite difficult conditions. Radnote sensors typically measure gamma radiation dose rates and are used for continuous environmental monitoring. 			how many sensors are working Ukraine now? 			In total, approximately 82 radiation monitoring devices are deployed in Ukraine within this dataset. This includes 78 Radnote (Notehub) sensors plus 4 bGeigie mobile or stationary devices. The sensors appear to be functioning normally and provide redundancy by offering multiple measurement points across the city. 			What is the latest reading 			The most recent readings from sensors in Mitsue, Nara (Japan) fall between 26 and 29 counts per minute (CPM), corresponding to approximately 0.18–0.20 µSv/h. What can you tell me about track 8fTWdz? 			Track 8fTWdz represents a mobile radiation survey conducted near Wolverine, Michigan (USA) on October 3, 2017. The survey collected 777 individual measurements while moving through the area. Recorded dose rates ranged from approximately 0.05 to 0.09 µSv/h, which corresponds to normal background radiation levels. Track 8iCb34 is a large mobile dataset collected around Ottawa, Canada on March 14, 2023. It includes approximately 9,388 measurements, implying a long survey route or high-frequency sampling. Radiation levels ranged from about 0.06 to 0.15 µSv/h, consistent with normal background. Such dense datasets are useful for high-resolution radiation mapping of urban and suburban environments. 			What is Safecast? 			Safecast is a global, volunteer-driven citizen science organization founded in response to the 2011 Fukushima Daiichi nuclear disaster. Its mission is to collect, analyze, and openly share environmental radiation data worldwide. Volunteers use portable detectors (such as bGeigie devices) and fixed sensors to gather measurements, which are published in an open database and visualized on public maps. Safecast promotes transparency, community empowerment, and independent environmental monitoring. Over time, its scope has expanded to include air quality and other environmental data streams. 			hello 			(No answer recorded.)

{
  "file_path": "/tmp/anytype-scan.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Scan AnyType space for all objects and identify duplicates.\"\"\"\nimport json, urllib.request\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nAPI_KEY = \"REDACTED\"\nSPACE_ID = \"bafyreig4q7t3vt7b7zmvfv3emj7jfrvjamuhu4crws3dhn3uaxhh3u37k4.10piockh34xft\"\nBASE = \"http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1\"\n\nall_objects = []\noffset = 0\nwhile True:\n    url = f\"{BASE}/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/search\"\n    data = json.dumps({\"query\": \"\", \"limit\": 100, \"offset\": offset}).encode()\n    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=\"POST\")\n    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {API_KEY}\")\n    req.add_header(\"Anytype-Version\", \"2025-11-08\")\n    req.add_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n    resp = urllib.request.urlopen(req)\n    result = json.loads(resp.read())\n    objs = result.get(\"data\", [])\n    all_objects.extend(objs)\n    if not result.get(\"pagination\", {}).get(\"has_more\", False):\n        break\n    offset += len(objs)\n\nprint(f\"Total objects: {len(all_objects)}\")\n\nby_name = defaultdict(list)\nfor o in all_objects:\n    name = o.get(\"name\", \"\") or \"(empty)\"\n    by_name[name].append(o)\n\nunnamed = by_name.pop(\"(empty)\", [])\nnamed_items = sorted(by_name.items())\n\nprint(f\"Named objects: {sum(len(v) for _, v in named_items)}\")\nprint(f\"Unnamed objects: {len(unnamed)}\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== NAMED OBJECTS ===\")\nfor name, objs in named_items:\n    dup_flag = f\" ** {len(objs)}x DUPLICATE **\" if len(objs) > 1 else \"\"\n    for o in objs:\n        print(f\"  {o['id'][:55]}  | {name}{dup_flag}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n=== UNNAMED OBJECTS (first 10 of {len(unnamed)}) ===\")\nfor o in unnamed[:10]:\n    snippet = o.get(\"snippet\", \"\")[:100] if o.get(\"snippet\") else \"no-snippet\"\n    print(f\"  {o['id'][:55]} | type={o.get('object','?')} | {snippet}\")\n"
}
