> AGENT

### Step 3: Clean up duplicates

I'll write a cleanup script that:
1. Gets all objects from the space
2. Uses the objectmap to identify the "canonical" version of each file
3. Groups unnamed objects by their snippet/content
4. Keeps one copy, deletes the rest

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01VSEf1KCYEhphuXmvZuVBzs
```json
{
  "file_path": "/tmp/anytype-cleanup.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAnyType Duplicate Cleanup Script\nIdentifies and removes duplicate objects from an AnyType space.\nKeeps objects tracked in the objectmap and one copy of each unique content.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport urllib.request\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nAPI_KEY = \"REDACTED\"\nSPACE_ID = \"bafyreig4q7t3vt7b7zmvfv3emj7jfrvjamuhu4crws3dhn3uaxhh3u37k4.10piockh34xft\"\nBASE = \"http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1\"\nOBJECTMAP_FILE = \"/root/.anytype-workspace-objectmap.json\"\n\nDRY_RUN = \"--dry-run\" in sys.argv or \"-n\" in sys.argv\nDELETE = \"--delete\" in sys.argv\n\ndef api_request(method, path, data=None):\n    url = f\"{BASE}{path}\"\n    if data:\n        body = json.dumps(data).encode()\n    else:\n        body = None\n    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=body, method=method)\n    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {API_KEY}\")\n    req.add_header(\"Anytype-Version\", \"2025-11-08\")\n    req.add_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n    resp = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60)\n    return json.loads(resp.read())\n\ndef get_all_objects():\n    \"\"\"Fetch all objects from the space, handling pagination.\"\"\"\n    all_objects = []\n    offset = 0\n    while True:\n        result = api_request(\"POST\", f\"/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/search\", {\n            \"query\": \"\", \"limit\": 100, \"offset\": offset\n        })\n        objs = result.get(\"data\", [])\n        all_objects.extend(objs)\n        print(f\"  Fetched {len(all_objects)} objects so far...\")\n        if not result.get(\"pagination\", {}).get(\"has_more\", False):\n            break\n        offset += len(objs)\n        time.sleep(0.5)  # Be gentle with the API\n    return all_objects\n\ndef delete_object(obj_id):\n    \"\"\"Delete an object from AnyType.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        api_request(\"DELETE\", f\"/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/objects/{obj_id}\")\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"    ERROR deleting {obj_id}: {e}\")\n        return False\n\ndef main():\n    if not DELETE and not DRY_RUN:\n        print(\"Usage: python3 anytype-cleanup.py [--dry-run | --delete]\")\n        print(\"  --dry-run  Show what would be deleted without actually deleting\")\n        print(\"  --delete   Actually delete duplicate objects\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    mode = \"DRY RUN\" if DRY_RUN else \"DELETE MODE\"\n    print(f\"=== AnyType Duplicate Cleanup ({mode}) ===\\n\")\n\n    # Load objectmap (known good object IDs)\n    try:\n        with open(OBJECTMAP_FILE) as f:\n            objectmap = json.load(f)\n        print(f\"Loaded objectmap: {len(objectmap)} entries\")\n        keep_ids = set(objectmap.values())\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: Could not load objectmap: {e}\")\n        objectmap = {}\n        keep_ids = set()\n\n    # Fetch all objects\n    print(\"\\nFetching all objects from space...\")\n    all_objects = get_all_objects()\n    print(f\"\\nTotal objects in space: {len(all_objects)}\")\n\n    # Separate into: kept (in objectmap), named (with names), unnamed\n    kept = []\n    candidates = []\n    for o in all_objects:\n        if o[\"id\"] in keep_ids:\n            kept.append(o)\n        else:\n            candidates.append(o)\n\n    print(f\"Objects in objectmap (KEEP): {len(kept)}\")\n    print(f\"Objects not in objectmap (candidates for cleanup): {len(candidates)}\")\n\n    # Group candidates by their snippet (content fingerprint)\n    by_snippet = defaultdict(list)\n    for o in candidates:\n        snippet = (o.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:200].strip()\n        if snippet:\n            by_snippet[snippet].append(o)\n        else:\n            by_snippet[\"__no_snippet__\"].append(o)\n\n    # For each group, check if an objectmap version exists with similar content\n    to_delete = []\n    to_keep_extra = []\n\n    for snippet, objs in by_snippet.items():\n        if snippet == \"__no_snippet__\":\n            # Objects with no content - likely empty/broken, delete all\n            to_delete.extend(objs)\n            continue\n\n        # Check if any objectmap entry has this content\n        has_objectmap_version = False\n        for kept_obj in kept:\n            kept_snippet = (kept_obj.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:200].strip()\n            if kept_snippet and kept_snippet[:100] == snippet[:100]:\n                has_objectmap_version = True\n                break\n\n        if has_objectmap_version:\n            # Objectmap has the canonical version, delete all duplicates\n            to_delete.extend(objs)\n        else:\n            # No objectmap version - keep one (first), delete rest\n            # These might be objects created by direct MongoDB writes\n            to_keep_extra.append(objs[0])\n            if len(objs) > 1:\n                to_delete.extend(objs[1:])\n\n    print(f\"\\n=== CLEANUP PLAN ===\")\n    print(f\"Objects to KEEP (objectmap): {len(kept)}\")\n    print(f\"Objects to KEEP (unique, no objectmap version): {len(to_keep_extra)}\")\n    print(f\"Objects to DELETE (duplicates): {len(to_delete)}\")\n\n    if to_keep_extra:\n        print(f\"\\n--- Extra objects being kept (not in objectmap) ---\")\n        for o in to_keep_extra:\n            snippet = (o.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:80].replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n            name = o.get(\"name\", \"\")\n            print(f\"  KEEP: {o['id'][:50]} | {name or snippet}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- Objects to delete ---\")\n    for o in to_delete:\n        snippet = (o.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:80].replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n        name = o.get(\"name\", \"\")\n        print(f\"  DEL:  {o['id'][:50]} | {name or snippet}\")\n\n    if DELETE and to_delete:\n        print(f\"\\n=== DELETING {len(to_delete)} objects ===\")\n        deleted = 0\n        failed = 0\n        for i, o in enumerate(to_delete):\n            snippet = (o.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:60].replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n            print(f\"  [{i+1}/{len(to_delete)}] Deleting {o['id'][:40]}... ({snippet})\")\n            if delete_object(o[\"id\"]):\n                deleted += 1\n            else:\n                failed += 1\n            time.sleep(0.3)  # Rate limit\n        print(f\"\\nDone! Deleted: {deleted}, Failed: {failed}\")\n    elif DRY_RUN:\n        print(f\"\\n(Dry run - no objects were deleted. Use --delete to actually delete.)\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{

> AGENT

The pagination total is actually **292 objects**, not 88K! The scan script got stuck in an infinite loop because the pagination was repeating. The objects.db is only 3MB which confirms ~292 objects.

Let me write a proper cleanup script.

id: toolu_012Gvhn3Yw7kcz6npi8pnVVy
```json
{
  "file_path": "/tmp/anytype-cleanup-v2.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAnyType Duplicate Cleanup v2 - Fixed pagination\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport urllib.request\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nAPI_KEY = \"REDACTED\"\nSPACE_ID = \"bafyreig4q7t3vt7b7zmvfv3emj7jfrvjamuhu4crws3dhn3uaxhh3u37k4.10piockh34xft\"\nBASE = \"http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1\"\nOBJECTMAP_FILE = \"/root/.anytype-workspace-objectmap.json\"\nMAX_OBJECTS = 500  # Safety limit\n\nDRY_RUN = \"--dry-run\" in sys.argv\nDELETE = \"--delete\" in sys.argv\n\ndef api_request(method, path, data=None):\n    url = f\"{BASE}{path}\"\n    body = json.dumps(data).encode() if data else None\n    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=body, method=method)\n    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {API_KEY}\")\n    req.add_header(\"Anytype-Version\", \"2025-11-08\")\n    req.add_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n    resp = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60)\n    return json.loads(resp.read())\n\ndef get_all_objects():\n    \"\"\"Fetch all objects, respecting pagination total.\"\"\"\n    all_objects = []\n    seen_ids = set()\n    offset = 0\n\n    # First request to get total\n    result = api_request(\"POST\", f\"/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/search\", {\"query\": \"\", \"limit\": 100, \"offset\": 0})\n    total = result.get(\"pagination\", {}).get(\"total\", 0)\n    print(f\"  Pagination reports total: {total}\")\n\n    objs = result.get(\"data\", [])\n    for o in objs:\n        if o[\"id\"] not in seen_ids:\n            seen_ids.add(o[\"id\"])\n            all_objects.append(o)\n\n    offset = len(objs)\n    while offset < total and offset < MAX_OBJECTS:\n        result = api_request(\"POST\", f\"/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/search\", {\"query\": \"\", \"limit\": 100, \"offset\": offset})\n        objs = result.get(\"data\", [])\n        if not objs:\n            break\n        new_count = 0\n        for o in objs:\n            if o[\"id\"] not in seen_ids:\n                seen_ids.add(o[\"id\"])\n                all_objects.append(o)\n                new_count += 1\n        if new_count == 0:\n            print(f\"  No new objects at offset {offset}, stopping\")\n            break\n        offset += len(objs)\n        print(f\"  Fetched {len(all_objects)} unique objects...\")\n        time.sleep(0.3)\n\n    return all_objects\n\ndef delete_object(obj_id):\n    try:\n        api_request(\"DELETE\", f\"/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/objects/{obj_id}\")\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: {e}\")\n        return False\n\ndef main():\n    if not DELETE and not DRY_RUN:\n        print(\"Usage: python3 anytype-cleanup-v2.py [--dry-run | --delete]\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    mode = \"DRY RUN\" if DRY_RUN else \"DELETE MODE\"\n    print(f\"=== AnyType Duplicate Cleanup v2 ({mode}) ===\\n\")\n\n    # Load objectmap\n    try:\n        with open(OBJECTMAP_FILE) as f:\n            objectmap = json.load(f)\n        keep_ids = set(objectmap.values())\n        print(f\"Objectmap: {len(objectmap)} entries ({len(keep_ids)} unique IDs)\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: {e}\")\n        objectmap = {}\n        keep_ids = set()\n\n    # Fetch all objects\n    print(\"\\nFetching objects...\")\n    all_objects = get_all_objects()\n    print(f\"\\nTotal unique objects: {len(all_objects)}\")\n\n    # Separate kept vs candidates\n    kept = [o for o in all_objects if o[\"id\"] in keep_ids]\n    candidates = [o for o in all_objects if o[\"id\"] not in keep_ids]\n\n    # Only look at \"note\" type objects (skip system objects like types, relations, etc.)\n    note_candidates = []\n    system_objects = []\n    for o in candidates:\n        obj_type = o.get(\"type\", {})\n        type_key = obj_type.get(\"key\", \"\") if isinstance(obj_type, dict) else \"\"\n        if type_key in (\"note\", \"page\", \"task\", \"\"):\n            note_candidates.append(o)\n        else:\n            system_objects.append(o)\n\n    print(f\"\\nObjects in objectmap (KEEP): {len(kept)}\")\n    print(f\"System objects (SKIP): {len(system_objects)}\")\n    print(f\"User notes/pages to evaluate: {len(note_candidates)}\")\n\n    # Group note candidates by snippet (first 150 chars)\n    by_content = defaultdict(list)\n    for o in note_candidates:\n        snippet = (o.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:150].strip()\n        by_content[snippet].append(o)\n\n    to_delete = []\n    to_keep_extra = []\n\n    for snippet, objs in by_content.items():\n        if not snippet:\n            # Empty objects - delete\n            to_delete.extend(objs)\n            continue\n\n        # Check if objectmap has a version with same content\n        has_objectmap = False\n        for k_obj in kept:\n            k_snippet = (k_obj.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:150].strip()\n            if k_snippet and k_snippet[:80] == snippet[:80]:\n                has_objectmap = True\n                break\n\n        if has_objectmap:\n            # Objectmap has canonical - delete all duplicates\n            to_delete.extend(objs)\n        else:\n            # Keep first, delete rest\n            to_keep_extra.append(objs[0])\n            to_delete.extend(objs[1:])\n\n    print(f\"\\n=== CLEANUP PLAN ===\")\n    print(f\"KEEP (objectmap): {len(kept)}\")\n    print(f\"KEEP (unique, not in objectmap): {len(to_keep_extra)}\")\n    print(f\"SKIP (system): {len(system_objects)}\")\n    print(f\"DELETE (duplicates): {len(to_delete)}\")\n\n    if to_keep_extra:\n        print(f\"\\n--- Extra objects to keep ---\")\n        for o in to_keep_extra:\n            s = (o.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:60].replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n            print(f\"  KEEP: {o['id'][:45]} | {o.get('name','') or s}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- Objects to delete ({len(to_delete)}) ---\")\n    for o in to_delete:\n        s = (o.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:60].replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n        print(f\"  DEL:  {o['id'][:45]} | {o.get('name','') or s}\")\n\n    if DELETE and to_delete:\n        print(f\"\\n=== DELETING {len(to_delete)} objects ===\")\n        deleted = failed = 0\n        for i, o in enumerate(to_delete):\n            s = (o.get(\"snippet\", \"\") or \"\")[:40].replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n            print(f\"  [{i+1}/{len(to_delete)}] {o['id'][:35]}... {s}\")\n            if delete_object(o[\"id\"]):\n                deleted += 1\n            else:\n                failed += 1\n            time.sleep(0.2)\n        print(f\"\\nDone! Deleted: {deleted}, Failed: {failed}\")\n    elif DRY_RUN:\n        print(f\"\\n(Dry run - use --delete to actually delete)\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

Track 8iCb34 is a large mobile dataset collected around Ottawa, Canada on March 14, 2023. It includes approximately 9,388 measurements, implying a long survey route or high-frequency sampling. What is Safecast? 			Safecast is a global, volunteer-driven citizen science organization founded in response to the 2011 Fukushima Daiichi nuclear disaster. Its mission is to collect, analyze, and openly share environmental radiation data worldwide. Volunteers use portable detectors (such as bGeigie devices) and fixed sensors to gather measurements, which are published in an open database and visualized on public maps. Safecast promotes transparency, community empowerment, and independent environmental monitoring. Over time, its scope has expanded to include air quality and other environmental data streams. 			hello 			(No answer recorded.)

  DEL:  REDACTED |  ###  			Question 			Detailed Answer 			how many radnotes are working in ukraine now? 			There are 78 Radnote sensors (Notehub-connected devices) deployed across Ukraine. These are compact IoT radiation monitors that automatically transmit measurements via cellular or satellite networks. The sensors are geographically distributed across western, central, eastern, and southern regions, providing broad situational awareness. Many of these devices reported data as recently as March 2026, indicating that a large portion of the network remains operational despite difficult conditions. Radnote sensors typically measure gamma radiation dose rates and are used for continuous environmental monitoring. 			how many sensors are working Ukraine now? 			In total, approximately 82 radiation monitoring devices are deployed in Ukraine within this dataset. This includes 78 Radnote (Notehub) sensors plus 4 bGeigie mobile or stationary devices. Of these, about 65 sensors transmitted data within the last 24 hours, suggesting they are actively functioning. The remaining devices reported data earlier — ranging from days to months ago — which could indicate intermittent connectivity, maintenance downtime, relocation, or decommissioning. The network provides both fixed monitoring and mobile survey capability. 			how many data points have been collected tagged #bgieiges4ukraine? 			No measurements were found associated with the exact tag #bgieiges4ukraine. This could mean several things: the tag may be misspelled, formatted differently (e.g., #bGeigie4Ukraine, bGeigie_s4_ukraine), stored as metadata rather than hashtags, or not indexed in the queried dataset. It is also possible that relevant data exists but is categorized under campaign names, device IDs, or project codes instead of free-text tags. Further investigation using alternate spellings or filtering by deployment region may yield results. 			is something installed near znpp? 			One Radnote radiation sensor is located in Kryvyi Rih, approximately 180–200 km northwest of the Zaporizhzhia Nuclear Power Plant (ZNPP). No sensors are recorded in the immediate vicinity of the plant itself within this dataset. Therefore, radiation conditions directly around ZNPP cannot be assessed from these devices alone. However, the Kryvyi Rih sensor provides regional background monitoring that could detect large-scale radiological anomalies affecting a wider area. 			what devices installed in Kharkiv city? 			There are three active Radnote sensors installed within Kharkiv. These devices continuously monitor ambient gamma radiation levels. Reported dose rates are approximately 0.22–0.23 µSv/h, which is within the range of typical natural background radiation for urban environments. The sensors appear to be functioning normally and provide redundancy by offering multiple measurement points across the city. 			What is the latest reading 			The most recent readings from sensors in Mitsue, Nara (Japan) fall between 26 and 29 counts per minute (CPM), corresponding to approximately 0.18–0.20 µSv/h. These values are consistent with normal environmental background radiation in the region. Minor fluctuations within this range are expected due to natural variations in cosmic radiation, soil composition, humidity, rainfall, and detector characteristics. 			What can you tell me about track 8fTWdz? 			Track 8fTWdz represents a mobile radiation survey conducted near Wolverine, Michigan (USA) on October 3, 2017. The survey collected 777 individual measurements while moving through the area. Recorded dose rates ranged from approximately 0.05 to 0.09 µSv/h, which corresponds to normal background radiation levels. No elevated or anomalous readings were observed, indicating typical environmental conditions at the time of measurement. Mobile tracks like this are often gathered using vehicle-mounted detectors such as bGeigie units. 			what this map shows? Track 8hVmGA is a mobile survey conducted in Ontario, Canada on May 3, 2019, containing 265 measurement points. Observed dose rates ranged from roughly 0.04 to 0.10 µSv/h, indicating stable natural background radiation. The absence of spikes or outliers suggests no localized contamination sources along the route. 			What can you tell me about track 8iCb34? 			Track 8iCb34 is a large mobile dataset collected around Ottawa, Canada on March 14, 2023. It includes approximately 9,388 measurements, implying a long survey route or high-frequency sampling. Radiation levels ranged from about 0.06 to 0.15 µSv/h, consistent with normal background. Such dense datasets are useful for high-resolution radiation mapping of urban and suburban environments. 			What is Safecast? 			Safecast is a global, volunteer-driven citizen science organization founded in response to the 2011 Fukushima Daiichi nuclear disaster. Its mission is to collect, analyze, and openly share environmental radiation data worldwide. ```json
{
  "file_path": "/tmp/daily-report-api.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/bash\n# VPS Daily Health Report — Creates/updates report in AnyType via HTTP API\n# Runs: Daily at 07:01 JST (22:01 UTC)\n# Replaces the old file-watcher approach\n\nset -e\n\n# AnyType HTTP API config\nAPI_KEY=\"REDACTED\"\nSPACE_ID=\"bafyreig4q7t3vt7b7zmvfv3emj7jfrvjamuhu4crws3dhn3uaxhh3u37k4.10piockh34xft\"\nAPI_URL=\"http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1\"\n\nDATE=$(date +'%Y-%m-%d')\nTIMESTAMP=$(date +'%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')\nREPORT_NAME=\"VPS Daily Report — $DATE\"\n\nlog() {\n    echo \"[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] $1\"\n}\n\nlog \"Generating daily report for $DATE\"\n\n# Gather system info\nUPTIME=$(uptime | sed 's/.*up //' | sed 's/,.*//')\nDISK=$(df -h / | tail -1 | awk '{print $3 \"/\" $2 \" (\" $5 \")\"}')\nMEMORY=$(free -h | grep Mem | awk '{print $3 \"/\" $2}')\nLOAD=$(cat /proc/loadavg | awk '{print $1 \", \" $2 \", \" $3}')\n\n# Service status\nNGINX_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active nginx 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\nFAIL2BAN_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active fail2ban 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\nANYTYPE_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active anytype-cli 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\nOPENCLAW_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active openclaw-gateway 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\nANYTYPE_SYNC_STATUS=$(systemctl is-active anytype.service 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\n\n# fail2ban stats\nBANNED_IPS=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null | grep \"Currently banned\" | awk '{print $NF}' || echo \"0\")\nTOTAL_BANNED=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null | grep \"Total banned\" | awk '{print $NF}' || echo \"0\")\n\n# Generate markdown content\nCONTENT=\"# VPS Daily Report — $DATE\n\n**Generated:** $TIMESTAMP\n\n---\n\n## System\n- **Uptime:** $UPTIME\n- **Disk:** $DISK\n- **Memory:** $MEMORY\n- **CPU Load:** $LOAD\n\n## Services\n| Service | Status |\n|---------|--------|\n| nginx | $NGINX_STATUS |\n| fail2ban | $FAIL2BAN_STATUS |\n| anytype-cli | $ANYTYPE_STATUS |\n| anytype-sync-server | $ANYTYPE_SYNC_STATUS |\n| openclaw-gateway | $OPENCLAW_STATUS |\n\n## Security\n- Banned IPs: $BANNED_IPS\n- Total banned: $TOTAL_BANNED\n\n---\n*Auto-generated via AnyType HTTP API*\"\n\n# Check if today's report already exists (search by name)\nlog \"Checking for existing report...\"\nSEARCH_RESULT=$(curl -s --max-time 10 \\\n    -H \"Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY\" \\\n    -H \"Anytype-Version: 2025-11-08\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    \"$API_URL/spaces/$SPACE_ID/search\" \\\n    -X POST \\\n    -d \"{\\\"query\\\":\\\"VPS Daily Report — $DATE\\\",\\\"limit\\\":5}\")\n\nEXISTING_ID=$(echo \"$SEARCH_RESULT\" | python3 -c \"\nimport json, sys\ntry:\n    data = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    for obj in data.get('data', []):\n        snippet = (obj.get('snippet','') or '')\n        if 'VPS Daily Report' in snippet and '$DATE' in snippet:\n            print(obj['id'])\n            break\nexcept: pass\n\" 2>/dev/null)\n\nif [ -n \"$EXISTING_ID\" ]; then\n    log \"Updating existing report: $EXISTING_ID\"\n    # Update existing object\n    RESULT=$(curl -s --max-time 15 \\\n        -H \"Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY\" \\\n        -H \"Anytype-Version: 2025-11-08\" \\\n        -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n        \"$API_URL/spaces/$SPACE_ID/objects/$EXISTING_ID\" \\\n        -X PATCH \\\n        -d \"{\\\"name\\\":\\\"$REPORT_NAME\\\",\\\"body\\\":$(echo \"$CONTENT\" | python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.dumps(sys.stdin.read()))')}\")\n    log \"Updated: $EXISTING_ID\"\nelse\n    log \"Creating new report...\"\n    # Create new object\n    BODY=$(python3 -c \"\nimport json\ncontent = '''$CONTENT'''\nprint(json.dumps({\n    'name': '$REPORT_NAME',\n    'object_type_unique_key': 'ot-note',\n    'body': content\n}))\n\")\n    RESULT=$(curl -s --max-time 15 \\\n        -H \"Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY\" \\\n        -H \"Anytype-Version: 2025-11-08\" \\\n        -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n        \"$API_URL/spaces/$SPACE_ID/objects\" \\\n        -X POST \\\n        -d \"$BODY\")\n\n    NEW_ID=$(echo \"$RESULT\" | python3 -c \"import json,sys; print(json.load(sys.stdin).get('object',{}).get('id','unknown'))\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\n    log \"Created: $NEW_ID\"\nfi\n\nlog \"Daily report complete\"\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/tmp/daily-report-api.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/bash\n# VPS Daily Health Report — Creates report in AnyType via HTTP API\n# Runs: Daily at 07:01 JST (22:01 UTC)\n\nset -e\n\nAPI_KEY=\"REDACTED\"\nSPACE_ID=\"bafyreig4q7t3vt7b7zmvfv3emj7jfrvjamuhu4crws3dhn3uaxhh3u37k4.10piockh34xft\"\nAPI_URL=\"http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1\"\nLOG_FILE=\"/var/log/anytype-daily-report.log\"\n\nDATE=$(date +'%Y-%m-%d')\nTIMESTAMP=$(date +'%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')\n\nlog() {\n    echo \"[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] $1\" | tee -a \"$LOG_FILE\"\n}\n\nlog \"Generating daily report for $DATE\"\n\n# Gather system info\nUPTIME=$(uptime | sed 's/.*up //' | sed 's/,.*//')\nDISK=$(df -h / | tail -1 | awk '{print $3 \"/\" $2 \" (\" $5 \")\"}')\nMEMORY=$(free -h | grep Mem | awk '{print $3 \"/\" $2}')\nLOAD=$(cat /proc/loadavg | awk '{print $1 \", \" $2 \", \" $3}')\n\n# Service status\nNGINX=$(systemctl is-active nginx 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\nFAIL2BAN=$(systemctl is-active fail2ban 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\nANYTYPE_CLI=$(systemctl is-active anytype-cli 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\nOPENCLAW=$(systemctl is-active openclaw-gateway 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\nANYTYPE_SYNC=$(systemctl is-active anytype.service 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\n\n# fail2ban stats\nBANNED=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null | grep \"Currently banned\" | awk '{print $NF}' || echo \"0\")\nTOTAL_BANNED=$(fail2ban-client status sshd 2>/dev/null | grep \"Total banned\" | awk '{print $NF}' || echo \"0\")\n\n# Build markdown body\nBODY=\"VPS Daily Report — $DATE\nGenerated: $TIMESTAMP\n\n---\n\nSystem\n- Uptime: $UPTIME\n- Disk: $DISK\n- Memory: $MEMORY\n- CPU Load: $LOAD\n\nServices\n- nginx: $NGINX\n- fail2ban: $FAIL2BAN\n- anytype-cli: $ANYTYPE_CLI\n- anytype-sync-server: $ANYTYPE_SYNC\n- openclaw-gateway: $OPENCLAW\n\nSecurity\n- Banned IPs: $BANNED\n- Total banned: $TOTAL_BANNED\n\n---\nAuto-generated via AnyType HTTP API\"\n\n# Create the report via API using python for JSON safety\nPAYLOAD=$(python3 -c \"\nimport json\nbody = '''${BODY}'''\nprint(json.dumps({'type_key': 'note', 'body': body}))\n\")\n\nRESULT=$(curl -s --max-time 15 \\\n    -H \"Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY\" \\\n    -H \"Anytype-Version: 2025-11-08\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    \"$API_URL/spaces/$SPACE_ID/objects\" \\\n    -X POST \\\n    -d \"$PAYLOAD\")\n\nOBJ_ID=$(echo \"$RESULT\" | python3 -c \"import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d.get('object',{}).get('id','ERROR'))\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"ERROR\")\n\nif [ \"$OBJ_ID\" = \"ERROR\" ]; then\n    log \"FAILED to create report: $RESULT\"\n    exit 1\nelse\n    log \"Created report: $OBJ_ID\"\nfi\n"
}
```

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of what was done:

## Summary

### Root Cause
The Go sync service (`anytype-workspace-sync`) had a bug in `api.go` - it always called `createObject()` instead of checking for existing objects first. Every service restart and file change created duplicates.

### What was fixed

1. **Stopped broken services** - Disabled `anytype-workspace-sync` and `anytype-watcher` (the old file-watcher approach)

2. **Enabled HTTP API** - Started `anytype-cli.service` which exposes both gRPC (31010) and HTTP API (31012). Enabled on boot.

3. **Cleaned up 60 duplicate objects** - Kept 27 objectmap entries + 13 unique objects, deleted 60 duplicates. Object count went from 292 to 233.

4. **Created new daily report script** - `/root/scripts/daily-report-api.sh` uses the AnyType HTTP API directly instead of writing files to a watched directory. No more duplicates.

5. **Set up cron job** - Runs daily at 22:01 UTC (07:01 JST): `1 22 * * * /root/scripts/daily-report-api.sh`

6. **Configured AnyType MCP** - Added `@anyproto/anytype-mcp` to OpenClaw's MCP config on the VPS, using the new `openclaw-vps` API key.

### Current state on VPS
| Component | Status |
|-----------|--------|
| anytype-cli (HTTP API + gRPC) | active, enabled |
| anytype (sync server) | active |
| openclaw-gateway | active |
| anytype-workspace-sync | disabled |
| anytype-watcher | disabled |
| AnyType MCP in OpenClaw | configured |
| Daily report cron | 22:01 UTC |
| API keys | `openclaw-vps` + `openclaw-laptop` |

     1→# Daily VPS Health Report Setup
     2→
     3→## Overview
     4→
     5→Automated daily VPS health reports generated at **07:00 JST (22:00 UTC)** and synced to AnyType.
     6→
     7→---
     8→
     9→## Setup Details
    10→
    11→### Cron Schedule
    12→```bash
    13→0 22 * * * /root/.openclaw/workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh >> /var/log/vps-daily-report.log 2>&1
    14→```
    15→
    16→- **Time:** 22:00 UTC every day
    17→- **JST equivalent:** 07:00 JST (next day)
    18→- **Script:** `/root/.openclaw/workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh`
    19→- **Logs:** `/var/log/vps-daily-report.log`
    20→
    21→---
    22→
    23→## What's in the Report
    24→
    25→**System Status:**
    26→- Uptime
    27→- Disk usage
    28→- Memory usage
    29→- CPU load average
    30→
    31→**Services:**
    32→- nginx (status)
    33→- fail2ban (status)
    34→- anytype-workspace-sync (status)
    35→- openclaw-gateway (status)

    45→---
    46→
    47→## Auto-Sync to AnyType
    48→
    49→The report file is automatically picked up by the file watcher:
    50→
    51→1. Script generates: `/root/anytype-workspace/VPS-DAILY-REPORT-2026-03-03.md`
    52→2. File watcher detects new file
    53→3. anytype-workspace-sync service syncs it
    54→4. Appears in AnyType workspace as: `VPS Daily Health Report — 2026-03-03`
    55→
    56→**Sync time:** <5 seconds after generation
    57→
    58→---

    93→Each report contains:

     5→Automated daily VPS health reports generated at **07:00 JST (22:00 UTC)** and synced to AnyType.
     6→
     7→---
     8→
     9→## Setup Details
    10→
    11→### Cron Schedule
    12→```bash
    13→0 22 * * * /root/.openclaw/workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh >> /var/log/vps-daily-report.log 2>&1
    14→```
    15→
    16→- **Time:** 22:00 UTC every day
    17→- **JST equivalent:** 07:00 JST (next day)
    18→- **Script:** `/root/.openclaw/workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh`
    19→- **Logs:** `/var/log/vps-daily-report.log`
    20→
    21→---
    22→
    23→## What's in the Report
    24→
    25→**System Status:**
    26→- Uptime
    27→- Disk usage
    28→- Memory usage
    29→- CPU load average
    30→
    31→**Services:**
    32→- nginx (status)
    33→- fail2ban (status)
    34→- anytype-workspace-sync (status)
    35→- openclaw-gateway (status)
    36→
    37→**Security:**
    38→- Currently banned IPs (fail2ban)
    39→- Total banned IPs (all-time)
    40→
    41→**Storage:**
    42→- AnyType workspace files count
    43→- Sync status
    44→
    45→---
    46→
    47→## Auto-Sync to AnyType
    48→
    49→The report file is automatically picked up by the file watcher:
    50→
    51→1. Script generates: `/root/anytype-workspace/VPS-DAILY-REPORT-2026-03-03.md`
    52→2. File watcher detects new file
    53→3. anytype-workspace-sync service syncs it
    54→4. Appears in AnyType workspace as: `VPS Daily Health Report — 2026-03-03`
    55→
    56→**Sync time:** <5 seconds after generation
    57→
    58→---
    59→
    60→## Testing
    61→
    62→To test the report manually:
    63→```bash
    64→bash /root/.openclaw/workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh
    65→```
    66→
    67→File will appear in:
    68→- `/root/anytype-workspace/VPS-DAILY-REPORT-YYYY-MM-DD.md`
    69→- AnyType workspace (within 5 seconds)
    70→
    71→---
    72→
    73→## Verification
    74→
    75→Check if cron job is scheduled:
    76→```bash
    77→crontab -l | grep vps-daily-report
    78→```
    79→
    80→Expected output:
    81→```
    82→0 22 * * * /root/.openclaw/workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh >> /var/log/vps-daily-report.log 2>&1
    83→```
    84→
    85→---
    86→
    87→## View Reports in AnyType
    88→
    89→**Location:** Your workspace
    90→**Name format:** `VPS Daily Health Report — YYYY-MM-DD`
    91→**Updated:** Every day at 07:00 JST
    92→
    93→Each report contains:
    94→- System metrics snapshot
    95→- Service status check
    96→- Security stats
    97→- Storage info
    98→
    99→---
   100→
   101→## Next Reports
   102→
   103→- **2026-03-04:** 07:00 JST
   104→- **2026-03-05:** 07:00 JST
   105→- ... and so on (daily)
   106→
   107→---
   108→
   109→## Maintenance
   110→
   111→No manual action needed. Reports are:
   112→- ✅ Auto-generated daily
   113→- ✅ Auto-synced to AnyType
   114→- ✅ Auto-logged for auditing
   115→
   116→If you need to modify:
   117→1. Edit `/root/.openclaw/workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh`
   118→2. Test: `bash /root/.openclaw/workspace/scripts/vps-daily-report.sh`
   119→3. Cron will use updated version next run
   120→

   124→**Status:** ✅ Active

```
     1→---
     2→name: anytype-sync
     3→description: "Query and monitor AnyType workspaces from OpenClaw via MongoDB. Use when: (1) listing workspace spaces, (2) querying object counts in spaces, (3) getting workspace summaries, (4) monitoring team activity in AnyType, (5) taking actions based on workspace data."
     4→---
     5→
     6→# AnyType Sync
     7→
     8→Query AnyType workspaces directly from OpenClaw. Perfect for team collaboration — monitor workspace changes and take automated actions via Slack or other channels.
     9→
    10→## Quick Start
    11→
    12→### 1. Prerequisites
    13→
    14→- AnyType running with MongoDB (localhost:27017)
    15→- Go binary compiled: `anytype-db` (pre-built in skill)
    16→- OpenClaw with Slack bot enabled (optional, for notifications)
    17→
    18→### 2. Basic Commands
    19→
    20→```bash
    21→# List all spaces
    22→anytype-db spaces
    23→
    24→# Get space summary (object count, recent activity)
    25→anytype-db summary <space-id>
    26→
    27→# Count objects in a space
    28→anytype-db count <space-id>
    29→
    30→# Get recent activity
    31→anytype-db activity <space-id>
    32→```
    33→
    34→### 3. Use in OpenClaw
    35→
    36→Add to your OpenClaw config or as a Slack command:
    37→
    38→```javascript
    39→// Query workspace
    40→const { execSync } = require('child_process');
    41→
    42→const result = execSync('/path/to/anytype-db spaces');
    43→console.log(result.toString());
    44→```
    45→
    46→## How It Works
    47→
    48→**Direct MongoDB Access:**
    49→
    50→The skill includes a compiled Go binary (`anytype-db`) that queries AnyType's MongoDB directly:
    51→
    52→```
    53→AnyType Workspace (MongoDB)
    54→    ↓
    55→anytype-db (Go CLI tool)
    56→    ↓
    57→OpenClaw
    58→    ↓
    59→Slack / Actions / Responses
    60→```
    61→
    62→**No APIs, no intermediaries.** Just direct database queries.
    63→
    64→## Available Commands
    65→
    66→### List Spaces
    67→
    68→```bash
    69→anytype-db spaces
    70→```
    71→
    72→Shows all workspaces available to the bot account:
    73→- Space ID (bafyrei...)
    74→- Identity (account that created it)
    75→- Shareable status
    76→
    77→**Example:**
    78→```
    79→23 spaces found:
    80→
    81→  ID: REDACTED.35fpfsusofs1o
    82→  Identity: REDACTED
    83→  Shareable: true
    84→```
    85→
    86→### Space Summary
    87→
    88→```bash
    89→anytype-db summary <space-id>
    90→```
    91→
    92→Get workspace overview:
    93→- Total objects in space
    94→- Recent activity
    95→- Last modified timestamp
    96→
    97→**Example output:**
    98→```json
    99→{
   100→  "spaceId": "bafyrei...",
   101→  "totalObjects": 42,
   102→  "recentActivity": [...],
   103→  "lastUpdated": "2026-02-28T14:00:00Z"
   104→}
   105→```
   106→
   107→### Count Objects
   108→
   109→```bash
   110→anytype-db count <space-id>
   111→```
   112→
   113→Get object count for a space (useful for monitoring changes):
   114→```
   115→42 payloads in space bafyrei...
   116→```
   117→
   118→### Recent Activity
   119→
   120→```bash
   121→anytype-db activity <space-id>
   122→```
   123→
   124→Show recent changes:
   125→```
   126→5 recent activities:
   127→
   128→  2026-02-28T14:00:00Z: page_created
   129→  2026-02-28T13:55:00Z: page_updated
   130→  ...
   131→```
   132→
   133→## Integration Examples
   134→
   135→### Slack Command Handler
   136→
   137→Add to your OpenClaw Slack bot:
   138→
   139→```javascript
   140→const { execSync } = require('child_process');
   141→
   142→async function handleSlackCommand(cmd, args) {
   143→  const binaryPath = '/path/to/anytype-db';
   144→  
   145→  if (cmd === 'spaces') {
   146→    const result = execSync(`${binaryPath} spaces`);
   147→    return result.toString();
   148→  }
   149→  
   150→  if (cmd === 'summary') {
   151→    const spaceId = args[0];
   152→    const result = execSync(`${binaryPath} summary ${spaceId}`);
   153→    const summary = JSON.parse(result.toString());
   154→    return `Space has ${summary.totalObjects} objects`;
   155→  }
   156→  
   157→  return 'Unknown command';
   158→}
   159→
   160→// Usage:
   161→// @openclaw anytype spaces
   162→// @openclaw anytype summary bafyrei...
   163→```
   164→
   165→### Monitor Workspace Changes
   166→
   167→```javascript
   168→const { execSync } = require('child_process');
   169→const cron = require('node-cron');
   170→
   171→const binaryPath = '/path/to/anytype-db';
   172→let previousCount = 0;
   173→
   174→// Check every 5 minutes
   175→cron.schedule('*/5 * * * *', () => {
   176→  const result = execSync(`${binaryPath} count <space-id>`);
   177→  const match = result.toString().match(/(\d+) payloads/);
   178→  const currentCount = parseInt(match[1]);
   179→  
   180→  if (currentCount > previousCount) {
   181→    const added = currentCount - previousCount;
   182→    console.log(`🎉 ${added} new objects added to workspace!`);
   183→    // Post to Slack, trigger action, etc.
   184→  }
   185→  
   186→  previousCount = currentCount;
   187→});
   188→```
   189→
   190→### Sync to Slack
   191→
   192→```javascript
   193→const { execSync } = require('child_process');
   194→const { WebClient } = require('@slack/web-api');
   195→
   196→const slack = new WebClient(process.env.SLACK_TOKEN);
   197→const binaryPath = '/path/to/anytype-db';
   198→
   199→async function postWorkspaceUpdate(spaceId, slackChannel) {
   200→  const result = execSync(`${binaryPath} summary ${spaceId}`);
   201→  const summary = JSON.parse(result.toString());
   202→  
   203→  await slack.chat.postMessage({
   204→    channel: slackChannel,
   205→    text: `📊 Workspace Update: ${summary.totalObjects} objects`,
   206→    blocks: [
   207→      {
   208→        type: 'section',
   209→        text: {
   210→          type: 'mrkdwn',
   211→          text: `*Workspace Activity*\n` +
   212→                `Objects: ${summary.totalObjects}\n` +
   213→                `Last updated: ${summary.lastUpdated}`
   214→        }
   215→      }
   216→    ]
   217→  });
   218→}
   219→```
   220→
   221→## Configuration
   222→
   223→### MongoDB Connection
   224→
   225→By default connects to: `mongodb://127.0.0.1:27017`
   226→
   227→Override with environment variable:
   228→
   229→```bash
   230→export MONGODB_URL=mongodb://user:pass@host:27017
   231→anytype-db spaces
   232→```
   233→
   234→### Binary Location
   235→
   236→The compiled `anytype-db` binary is located at:
   237→```
   238→~/.openclaw/workspace/skills/anytype-sync/cmd/anytype-db/main.go
   239→```
   240→
   241→Compiled binary path (after building):
   242→```
   243→~/.openclaw/workspace/skills/anytype-sync/anytype-db
   244→```
   245→
   246→## Building from Source
   247→
   248→```bash
   249→cd ~/.openclaw/workspace/skills/anytype-sync
   250→
   251→# Requires Go 1.21+
   252→go mod tidy
   253→go build -o anytype-db ./cmd/anytype-db
   254→
   255→# Test it
   256→./anytype-db spaces
   257→```
   258→
   259→## Troubleshooting
   260→
   261→### "Connection refused"
   262→MongoDB not running. Start AnyType:
   263→```bash
   264→anytype serve
   265→```
   266→
   267→### "23 spaces found" but empty
   268→AnyType needs a running instance. The bot account must be logged in:
   269→```bash
   270→anytype auth status
   271→```
   272→
   273→### Binary not found
   274→Compile it first:
   275→```bash
   276→cd skills/anytype-sync
   277→go build -o anytype-db ./cmd/anytype-db
   278→```
   279→
   280→## Architecture
   281→
   282→```
   283→┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
   284→│  AnyType Desktop/Web UI             │
   285→│  (Team creates/edits pages)         │
   286→└────────────┬────────────────────────┘
   287→             │
   288→             ↓
   289→┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
   290→│  MongoDB (localhost:27017)          │
   291→│  - Stores spaces, objects, activity │
   292→└────────────┬────────────────────────┘
   293→             │
   294→             ↓
   295→┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
   296→│  anytype-db (Go CLI)                │
   297→│  Direct MongoDB queries             │
   298→└────────────┬────────────────────────┘
   299→             │
   300→             ↓
   301→┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
   302→│  OpenClaw                           │
   303→│  - Query workspaces                 │
   304→│  - Monitor changes                  │
   305→│  - Post to Slack                    │
   306→│  - Take automated actions           │
   307→└─────────────────────────────────────┘
   308→```
   309→
   310→## API Reference
   311→
   312→See `scripts/anytype-db/main.go` for the source code.
   313→
   314→**Available Go functions:**
   315→
   316→```go
   317→db := New("mongodb://127.0.0.1:27017")
   318→defer db.Disconnect()
   319→
   320→spaces, _ := db.ListSpaces()
   321→space, _ := db.GetSpace(spaceID)
   322→count, _ := db.CountPayloads(spaceID)
   323→summary, _ := db.GetSpaceSummary(spaceID)
   324→activity, _ := db.GetRecentActivity(spaceID, limit)
   325→```
   326→
   327→## Slack Integration
   328→
   329→Use the AnyType skill directly from Slack! See `slack/SETUP.md` for integration.
   330→
   331→**Quick example:**
   332→
   333→```
   334→@openclaw anytype spaces
   335→→ Lists all your workspaces
   336→
   337→@openclaw anytype summary <space-id>
   338→→ Shows object count and activity
   339→
   340→@openclaw anytype activity <space-id>
   341→→ Recent changes in workspace
   342→```
   343→
   344→Responses are formatted as beautiful Slack blocks for easy reading.
   345→
   346→## Next Steps
   347→
   348→- See `slack/SETUP.md` for Slack bot integration
   349→- See `references/setup-guide.md` for bot account setup
   350→- See `references/examples.md` for integration patterns
   351→- See `cmd/anytype-db/main.go` for source code
   352→
   353→## Support

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/DAILY-VPS-REPORT-SETUP.md",
  "content": "# Daily VPS Health Report Setup\n\n## Overview\n\nAutomated daily VPS health reports generated at **07:01 JST (22:01 UTC)** and pushed to AnyType via the HTTP API.\n\n**Architecture:** Uses the AnyType HTTP API directly (no file watcher). See `ANYTYPE-MCP-ARCHITECTURE.mmd` for the full diagram.\n\n---\n\n## Setup Details\n\n### Cron Schedule\n```bash\n1 22 * * * /root/scripts/daily-report-api.sh >> /var/log/anytype-daily-report.log 2>&1\n```\n\n- **Time:** 22:01 UTC every day\n- **JST equivalent:** 07:01 JST (next day)\n- **Script:** `/root/scripts/daily-report-api.sh`\n- **Logs:** `/var/log/anytype-daily-report.log`\n\n---\n\n## How It Works\n\n1. Cron fires `daily-report-api.sh` at 22:01 UTC\n2. Script collects system metrics (uptime, disk, memory, CPU, services, security)\n3. Script POSTs a new note to AnyType via HTTP API (`POST /v1/spaces/{id}/objects`)\n4. Report appears in AnyType space \"Rob New Place\"\n\n```\nCron → daily-report-api.sh → AnyType HTTP API (:31012) → AnyType Space\n```\n\nNo file watcher, no gRPC, no MongoDB writes. Just a direct API call.\n\n---\n\n## What's in the Report\n\n**System Status:**\n- Uptime\n- Disk usage\n- Memory usage\n- CPU load average\n\n**Services:**\n- nginx (status)\n- fail2ban (status)\n- anytype-cli (status)\n- openclaw-gateway (status)\n\n**Security:**\n- Currently banned IPs (fail2ban)\n- Total banned IPs (all-time)\n\n---\n\n## AnyType API Details\n\n- **Endpoint:** `http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/objects`\n- **Space ID:** `bafyreig4q7t3vt7b7zmvfv3emj7jfrvjamuhu4crws3dhn3uaxhh3u37k4.10piockh34xft`\n- **API Key:** Stored in script as `openclaw-vps` key\n- **Object type:** `note` (uses `type_key` field)\n- **Content:** Passed in `body` field as markdown\n\n---\n\n## Testing\n\nTo test the report manually:\n```bash\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'bash /root/scripts/daily-report-api.sh'\n```\n\nCheck the log:\n```bash\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'tail -20 /var/log/anytype-daily-report.log'\n```\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\nCheck if cron job is scheduled:\n```bash\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'crontab -l | grep daily-report'\n```\n\nCheck if AnyType HTTP API is running:\n```bash\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'ss -tlnp | grep 31012'\n```\n\nCheck service status:\n```bash\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'systemctl status anytype-cli.service'\n```\n\n---\n\n## VPS Services (Current State)\n\n| Service | Status | Purpose |\n|---------|--------|---------|\n| `anytype-cli.service` | **active, enabled** | Runs `anytype serve -q` (gRPC :31010 + HTTP :31012) |\n| `anytype-workspace-sync.service` | disabled | Old Go file watcher (replaced by MCP/API) |\n| `anytype-watcher.service` | disabled | Old file watcher (replaced) |\n\n---\n\n## Migration Notes (March 2026)\n\nThe old setup used a Go file-watcher service (`anytype-workspace-sync`) that watched `/root/anytype-workspace/` and synced via gRPC. This had a critical bug: it always created new objects instead of updating existing ones, causing duplicates on every restart.\n\n**Old flow:** Script → write file → file watcher → gRPC → AnyType (broken, created duplicates)\n**New flow:** Script → HTTP API → AnyType (works correctly)\n\nCleanup performed: 60 duplicate objects deleted from AnyType space.\n\n---\n\n**Setup Date:** 2026-03-26 (migrated from file-watcher approach)\n**Status:** Active\n"
}
```

Your setup is yours. Keeping them apart means you can update skills without losing your notes, and share skills without leaking your infrastructure.\n\n---\n\nAdd whatever helps you do your job. This is your cheat sheet.\n"

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/openclaw-workspace/skills/anytype-sync/SKILL.md",
  "content": "---\nname: anytype-sync\ndescription: \"Interact with AnyType workspaces via MCP and HTTP API. Use when: (1) creating or updating pages in AnyType, (2) querying objects in a space, (3) managing daily reports, (4) searching AnyType content, (5) cleaning up duplicate objects.\"\n---\n\n# AnyType Sync\n\nInteract with AnyType workspaces from OpenClaw via the official AnyType MCP server or direct HTTP API calls.\n\n## Architecture\n\nSee `ANYTYPE-MCP-ARCHITECTURE.mmd` for the full architecture diagram.\n\n```\nOpenClaw / Claude Code\n    ↓ MCP tools\n@anyproto/anytype-mcp\n    ↓ HTTP REST\nAnyType HTTP API (:31012)\n    ↓\nAnyType Space (synced to all devices)\n```\n\n### Two Access Methods\n\n1. **MCP (preferred)** — `@anyproto/anytype-mcp` provides structured tools for Claude/OpenClaw\n2. **HTTP API (scripts/cron)** — Direct REST calls for automation (daily reports, cleanup)\n\n## MCP Setup\n\n### On Laptop (via SSH tunnel)\n\nStart the SSH tunnel:\n```bash\nssh -L 31012:127.0.0.1:31012 root@65.108.24.131\n```\n\nMCP is configured in `.claude/settings.local.json`:\n```json\n{\n  \"mcpServers\": {\n    \"anytype\": {\n      \"command\": \"npx\",\n      \"args\": [\"-y\", \"@anthropic-ai/anytype-mcp\"],\n      \"env\": {\n        \"ANYTYPE_API_URL\": \"http://127.0.0.1:31012\",\n        \"ANYTYPE_API_KEY\": \"<laptop-api-key>\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### On VPS\n\nMCP is configured in `/root/.openclaw/openclaw.json` with the `openclaw-vps` API key.\n\n## HTTP API Reference\n\n### Authentication\n\nAll requests require:\n```\nAuthorization: Bearer <API_KEY>\nAnytype-Version: 2025-11-08\nContent-Type: application/json\n```\n\n### Endpoints\n\n**Search objects:**\n```bash\ncurl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/search \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY\" \\\n  -H \"Anytype-Version: 2025-11-08\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"query\": \"\", \"limit\": 100, \"offset\": 0}'\n```\n\n**Create a note:**\n```bash\ncurl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/objects \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY\" \\\n  -H \"Anytype-Version: 2025-11-08\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"name\": \"Title\", \"type_key\": \"note\", \"body\": \"Markdown content\"}'\n```\n\n**Delete an object:**\n```bash\ncurl -X DELETE http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1/spaces/{SPACE_ID}/objects/{OBJECT_ID} \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY\" \\\n  -H \"Anytype-Version: 2025-11-08\"\n```\n\n### API Key Management\n\n```bash\n# Create a new API key\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'anytype auth apikey create --name my-key'\n\n# List API keys\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'anytype auth apikey list'\n```\n\n## Scripts\n\n### daily-report-api.sh (VPS)\n\nGenerates and pushes a daily health report to AnyType:\n- Located at `/root/scripts/daily-report-api.sh`\n- Runs via cron at 22:01 UTC (07:01 JST)\n- Creates a note with system metrics, service status, and security info\n\n### anytype-api.js\n\nNode.js HTTP API client with convenience methods:\n- `createPage()`, `updatePage()`, `getPage()`, `queryPages()`, `deletePage()`\n- Located at `scripts/anytype-api.js`\n\n### Cleanup Scripts\n\nPython scripts for identifying and removing duplicate objects:\n- Scan: `anytype-scan.py` — lists all objects, groups by name\n- Cleanup: `anytype-cleanup.py` — removes duplicates, keeps objectmap entries\n\n## Legacy (Deprecated)\n\nThe following are no longer in use:\n\n- **anytype-workspace-sync** (Go service) — File watcher that synced via gRPC. Had a bug where it always created new objects instead of updating. Replaced by MCP + HTTP API.\n- **anytype-db** (Go CLI) — Direct MongoDB queries. Replaced by HTTP API.\n- **anytype-watcher** — Systemd file watcher. Replaced by cron + API.\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### \"Connection refused\" on HTTP API\nAnyType CLI not running:\n```bash\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'systemctl status anytype-cli.service'\nssh root@65.108.24.131 'systemctl restart anytype-cli.service'\n```\n\n### SSH tunnel not working\n```bash\nssh -L 31012:127.0.0.1:31012 root@65.108.24.131\n# Verify: curl http://127.0.0.1:31012/v1/spaces -H \"Authorization: Bearer $KEY\" -H \"Anytype-Version: 2025-11-08\"\n```\n\n### MCP not connecting\nCheck that the SSH tunnel is active and the API key is correct.\n\n## Support\n\n- GitHub: https://github.com/robouden/openclaw-workspace\n- Issues: https://github.com/robouden/openclaw-workspace/issues\n"
}
```
